jamadagniḥ kauśikaś ca vāsukiḥ kaṅkaṇī karaḥ
takṣakaś ca tathā nāga elāpatras tathā dvijāḥ
śaṅkhapālas tathā cānyastvairāvata iti smṛtaḥ
hāhā hūhūrmuniśreṣṭhā viśvāvasuranuttamaḥ
citraseno mahātejāścorṇāyuścaiva suvratāḥ
dhṛtarāṣṭraḥ sūryavarcā devī sākṣātkṛtasthalā
śubhānanā śubaśroṇirdivyā vai puñjikasthalā
pūrvacittiriti khyātā devī sākṣāttilottamā
raṃbhā cāṃbhojavadanā rathakṛdgrāmaṇīḥ śubhaḥ
varuṇaś ca tathaivānyaḥ suṣeṇaḥ senajicchubhaḥ
sarpo vyāghraḥ punaścāpo vāto vidyuddivākaraḥ
dhātrādiviṣṇuparyantā devā dvādaśa kīrtitāḥ
ādityaṃ paramaṃ bhānuṃ bhābhirāpyāyayanti te
nāgāścāśvatarāntāstu vāsukipramukhāḥ śubhāḥ
dvādaśaiva mahādevaṃ vahantyevaṃ yatākramam
krameṇa sūryavarcāntāstuṃburu pramukhāṃbupam
gītairenamupāsaṃte gandharvā dvādaśottamāḥ
kṛtasthalādyā raṃbhāntā divyāścāpsaraso ravim
tāṇḍavaiḥ sarasaiḥ sarvāścopāsaṃte yathākramam
divyāḥ satyajidantāś ca grāmaṇyo rathakṛnmukhāḥ
dvādaśāsya krameṇaiva kurvatebhīṣusaṃgraham
prayānti yajñopetāntā rakṣohetimukhāḥ saha
sāyudhā dvādaśaivaite rākṣasāścā yathākramam
subāhunāmā grāmaṇyau rathacitraś ca tāvubhau
dhanañjaya irāvāṃś ca suruciḥ saparāvasuḥ
ghṛtācī cāpsaraḥ śreṣṭhā viśvācī cātiśobhanā
brahmopetas tathā rakṣo yajñopetaś ca yaḥ smṛtaḥ
saṃkṣepādvistarāccaiva yatāvṛttaṃ yathāśrutam
sūta uvāca
kramate śuklapakṣādau bhāskarātparamāsthitaḥ
sūta uvāca
vāritejomayaścātha piśaṅgaiścaiva śobhanaiḥ
daśabhiścākṛśairaśvairnānāvarṇai rathaḥ smṛtaḥ
śukrasya kṣmāmayairyukto daityācāryasya dhīmataḥ
pādahīnau vakrasaurī tathā'yāmapramāmataḥ
taṃ viṣṇulokaṃ paramaṃ jñātvā mucyeta kilbiṣāt
ṛtavaḥ ṣaṭ smṛtāḥ sarve samāgacchanti pañcadhā
evaṃ saṃkṣipya kathitaṃ grahāṇaāṃ gamanaṃ dvijāḥ
abhiṣiktaḥ sahasrāṃśū rudreṇa tu yathā guhaḥ
tasmādgrahārcanā kāryā agnau codyaṃ yatāvidhi
ādityagraha pīḍāyāṃ sadbhiḥ kāryārthasiddhaye
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
abhyaṣiñcatkathaṃ brahmā cādhipatye prajāpatiḥ
sūta uvāca
ṛkṣāṇāmoṣadhīnāṃ ca somaṃ brahmā prajāpatiḥ
apāṃ ca varuṇaṃ deva dhanānāṃ yakṣapuṅgavam
dharmaṃ pitṝṇām adhipaṃ nirṛtiṃ piśitāsinām
rudraṃ paśūnāṃ bhūtānāṃ nandīnāṃ gaṇanāyakam
vīrāṇā varibhadraṃ ca piśācānāṃ bhayaṅkaram
vighnānāṃ vyomajaṃ devaṃ gajasyaṃ tu vināyakam
strīṇāṃ devīmumādevīṃ vacasāṃ ca sarasvatīm
viṣṇuṃ māyāvināṃ caiva svatmānaṃ jagatāṃ tathā
himavantaṃ girīṇāṃ tu nadīnāṃ caiva jāhnavīm
samudrāṇāṃ ca sarveṣāmadhipaṃ payasāṃ nidhim
vṛkṣāṇāṃ caiva cāśvatthaṃ plakṣaṃ ca prapitāmahaḥ
siṃhaṃ mṛgāṇāṃ vṛṣabhaṃ gāvāṃ ca mṛgādhipānāṃ śarabhaṃ cakāra
senādhipānāṃ guhamaprameyaṃ śrutīsmṛtīnāṃ lakulīśamīśam
abhyaṣiñcatsudharmāṇaṃ tathā śaṅkhāpadaṃ diśām
pṛthivyāṃ pṛthumīśānaṃ sarveṣāṃ tu maheśvaram
caturmūrtiṣu sarvajñaṃ śaṅkaraṃ vṛṣabhadhvajam
prasādādbhagavāñchambhoścābhyaṣiñcadyathākramam
purābhiṣicya puṇyātmā rarāja bhuvaneśvaraḥ
abhiṣiktāstatastvete viśiṣṭā viśvayoninā
sūta uvāca
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
jale cābjaḥ samāviṣṭo nādbhir āgniḥ praśāmyati
saptabhistapate mitrastvaṣṭā cauvāṣṭabhiḥ smṛtaḥ
nakṣatrādhipatiḥ somo nayanaṃ vāmamīśituḥ
nayanaṃ caiva mīśasya dakṣiṇaṃ bhāskaraḥ svayam
teṣāṃ janānāṃ lokesminnayanaṃ nayate yataḥ
sūta uvāca
jagatpratāpanamṛte bhāskaraṃ rudrarūpiṇam
dṛśyante divi tāḥ sarvāḥ viśvaṃ cedaṃ punarjagat
sūta uvāca
abhimānino 'vatiṣṭhante devāḥ sthānaṃ punaḥ punaḥ
pūrvameva samākhyātā gatisteṣāṃ yathākramam
pañcaite hetavo jñeyā jyotirmānāvinirṇaye
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
kathaṃ viṣṇoḥ prasādādvai dhruvo buddhimatāṃ varaḥ
meḍhībhūto grāhāṇāṃ vai vaktumarhasi sāṃpratam
sūta uvāca
etamarthaṃ mayā pṛṣṭo nānāśāstraviśāradaḥ
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ purā prāha mahyaṃ śuśrūṣave dvijāḥ
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca
uttānapādo rājā vai pālayāmāsa medinīm
agrajāyāmabhūtputraḥ sunītyāṃ tu mahā yaśāḥ
dhruvo nāma mahāprājñaḥ kuladīpo mahāmatiḥ
surucistaṃ vinirdhūya svaputraṃ prītimānasā
nyaveśayattaṃ viprendrā hyaṅkaṃ rūpema mānitā
alabdhvā sa piturdhīmānaṅkaṃ duḥkhitamānasaḥ
mātuḥ samīpamāgamya ruroda sa punaḥ punaḥ
rudantaṃ putramāhedaṃ mātā śokapariplutā
surucirdayitā bhartustasyāḥ putropi tādṛśaḥ
mama tvaṃ mandabhāgyāyā jātaḥ putropyabhāgyavān
kiṃ śocasi kimarthaṃ tvaṃ rodamānaḥ punaḥ punaḥ
santaptahṛdayo bhūtvā mama śokaṃ kariṣyasi
svasthasthānaṃ dhruvaṃ putra svasaktyā tvaṃ samāpnuyāḥ
viśvāmitraṃ tato dṛṣṭvā praṇipatya yathāvidhi
sarveṣāmuparisthānaṃ kena prāpsyāmi sattama
pituraṅke samāsīnaṃ mātā māṃ surucirmune
vyadhūnayatsa taṃ rājā pitā novāca kiñcana
etasmātkāraṇādbrahyaṃstrastohaṃ mātaraṃ gataḥ
sunītirāha me mātā mākṛthāḥ śokamuttamam
tasyā hi vacanaṃ śrutvā sthānaṃ tava mahāmune
prāpto vanamidaṃ brahmannadya tvāṃ dṛṣṭavānprabho
rājaputra śṛṇuṣvedaṃ sthānamuttamamāpsyasi
dakṣiṇāṅgabhavaṃ śaṃbhormahādevasya dhīmataḥ
iṣṭadaṃ paramaṃ śuddhaṃ pavitramamalaṃ param
dhyāyansanātanaṃ viṣṇuṃ japahomaparāyaṇaḥ
prāṅmukho niyato bhūtvā jajāpa prītamānasaḥ
vetālā rākṣasā ghorāḥ siṃhādyāś ca mahāmṛgāḥ
tamabhyayurmahātmānaṃ buddhimohāya bhīṣaṇāḥ
sunīti rasya yā mātā tasyā rūpeṇa saṃvṛtā
piśāci samanuprāptā ruroda bhṛśaduḥkhatā
māmanāthāmapahāya tapa āsthitavānasi
evamādīni vākyāni bhāṣamāṇāṃ mahātapāḥ
anirīkṣyaiva hṛṣṭātmā harernāma jajāpa saḥ
āyayau bhagavānviṣṇuḥ dhruvantikamarātihā
tataḥ sa paramaṃ jñānamavāpya kuruṣottamam
tuṣṭāva prāñjalirbhūtvā sarvalokeśvaraṃ harim
sthānaṃ dhruvaṃ samāsādya dyotiṣāmagrabhugbhava
mātrā tvaṃ sahitastatra jyotiṣāṃ sthānamāpnuhi
tapasārādhya deveśaṃ purā labdhaṃ hi śaṅkarāt
viṣṇorājñāṃ puraskṛtya jyotiṣāṃ sthānamāptavān
evaṃ dhruvo mahātejā dvādaśākṣaravidyayā
sūta uvāca
tasmādyo vāsudevāya praṇāmaṃ kurute naraḥ
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
sūta uvāca
kadrūstviṣā danustadvattāsāṃ putrānvadāmi vaḥ
saudāminīṃ tathā kanyāṃ sarvalokabhayaṅkarīm
ete tu kāśyapeyāś ca saṃkṣepātparikīrtitāḥ
mānavasya nariṣyantaḥ putra āsīd damaḥ kila
bṛhaspateḥ śubhā kanyā nāmnā vai devavarṇinī
puṣpotkaṭā hyajanayatputrāṃstasmāddvijottamāḥ
vibhīṣaṇotiśuddhātmā dharmajñaḥ parikīrtitaḥ
atreḥ patnyo daśaivāsan suṃdaryaś ca pativratāḥ
balābalā ca viprendrā yā ca gopābalā smṛtā
śyāvaś ca pratvasaścaiva vavalguścātha gahvaraḥ
rakṣasā bhakṣite śaktau rudhireṇa tu vai tadā
śvetaḥ kṛṣṇaś ca gauraś ca śyāmo dhūmrastathāruṇaḥ
nīlo bādarikaścaiva sarve caite parāśarāḥ
trilokadhāraṇe śaktā devarṣikulasaṃbhavāḥ
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
vāsiṣṭho vadatāṃ śreṣṭha sūta vaktumihārhasi
sūta uvāca
rākṣaso rudhiro nāma vasiṣṭhasya sutaṃ purā
śaktiṃ sa bhakṣayāmāsa śakteḥ śāpātsahānujaiḥ
vasiṣṭhayājyaṃ viprendrāstadā diśyaiva bhūpatim
kalmāṣapādaṃ rudhiro viśvāmitreṇa coditaḥ
bhakṣitaḥ sa iti śrutvā vasiṣṭhastena rakṣasā
śaktiḥ śaktimatāṃ śreṣṭho bhrātṛbhiḥ saha dharmavit
naṣṭaṃ kulamiti śrutvā martuṃ cakre matiṃ tadā
smaranputraśataṃ caiva śaktijyeṣṭhaṃ ca śaktimān
na taṃ vināhaṃ jīviṣye iti niścitya duḥkhitaḥ
dharādharasyaiva tadā dharāyāṃ papāta patnyā sahasāśrudṛṣṭiḥ
dharādharāttaṃ patitaṃ dharā tadā dadhāra tatrāpi vicitrakaṇṭhī
karāṃbujābhyāṃ karikhelagāminī rudantamādāya ruroda sā ca
tadā tasya snuṣā prāha patnī śaktermahāmunim
vasiṣṭhaṃ vadatāṃ śreṣṭhaṃ rudantī bhayavihvalā
bhagavanbrāhmaṇaśreṣṭha tava devamidaṃ śubham
evamuktvātha dharmajñā karābhyāṃ kamalekṣaṇā
utthāpya śvaśuraṃ natvā netre saṃmṛjya vāriṇā
duḥkhitāpi paritrātuṃ śvaśuraṃ duḥkhitaṃ tadā
arundhatīṃ ca kalyāṇīṃ prārthayāmāsa duḥkhitām
snuṣāvākyaṃ tataḥ śrutvā vasiṣṭhotthāya bhūtalāt
saṃjñāmavāpya cāliṅgya sā papāta suduḥkhitā
aṃrudhatī kārabyāṃ tāṃ saṃspṛśyāsrākulekṣaṇām
ruroda muniśārdūlo bhāyaḥyā sutavatsalaḥ
atha nābyaṃbuje viṣṇoryathā tasyāścaturmukhaḥ
āsīno garbhaśayyāyāṃ kumāra ṛcamāha saḥ
tato niśamya bhagavānvasiṣṭha ṛcamādarāt
kenoktamiti saṃcintya tadātiṣṭhatsamāhitaḥ
vasiṣṭhamāha viśvātmā ghṛṇayā sa ghṛṇānidhiḥ
tava pautramukhāṃbhojādṛgeṣādya viniḥsṛtā
tataḥ praṇamya śirasā vasiṣṭho vārijekṣaṇam
adṛśyantyā mahātejāḥ pasparśodaramādarāt
lalāpārundhatī prekṣya tadāsau rudatīṃ dvijāḥ
tava putramimaṃ dṛṣṭvā bho śakte kuladhāraṇam
sūta uvāca
evamuktvā rudanvipra āliṅgyārundhatīṃ tadā
papāta tāḍayantīva svasya kukṣī karema vai
adṛsyantī jaghānātha śaktijasyālayaṃ śubhā
svodaraṃ duḥkhitā bhūmau lalāpa ca papāta ca
arundhati tadā bhītā vasiṣṭhaś ca mahāmatiḥ
samutthāpya snuṣāṃ bālāmūcaturbhayavihvalau
tavātmajaṃ śaktisutaṃ ca dṛṣṭvā cāsvādya vaktrāmṛtamāryasūnoḥ
trātuṃ yato dehamimaṃ munīndraḥ suniścitaḥ pāhi tataḥ śarīram
sūta uvāca
evaṃ snuṣāmupālabhya muniṃ cārundhatī sthitā
jīvitaṃ rakṣa dehasya dhātrī ca kuru yāddhitam
adṛśyanti uvāca
mayā yadi muniśreṣṭho trātuṃ vai niścitaṃ svakam
mamāśubhaṃ śubhaṃ dehaṃ kathañcitpālayāmyaham
priyaduḥkhamahaṃ prāpta hyasatī nātra saṃśayaḥ
mune duḥkhādahaṃ dagdhā yataḥ putrī mune tava
ahodbhutaṃ mayā dṛṣṭaṃ duḥkhapātrī hyahaṃ vibho
tathāpi bhartṛrahitā dīnā nāri bhavediha
pāhi māṃ tata āryendra paribhūtā bhaviṣyati
pitā mātā ca putrāsca pautrāḥ śvaśura eva ca
ete na bāndhavāḥ strīṇāṃ bhartā bandhuḥ parā gatiḥ
tadapyatra mṛṣā hyāsīd gataḥ śaktirahaṃ sthitā
patiṃ prāṇasamaṃ tyaktvā sthitā yatra kṣaṇaṃ yataḥ
vasiṣṭhāśvatthamāśritya hyamṛtā tu yathā latā
nirmūlāpyamṛtā bhartrātyaktā dīnā sthitāpyaham
snuṣā vākyaṃ niśamyaiva vasiṣṭho bhāryayā saha
tadā cakre matiṃ dhīmān yātuṃ svāśramamāśramī
kṛcchrātsabhāryo bhagavānvāsiṣṭhaḥ svāśramaṃ kṣaṇāt
adṛśyantyā ca puṇyātmā saṃviveśa sa cintayan
sā garbhaṃ pālayāmāsa kathacinmunipuṅgavāḥ
kulasaṃdhāraṇārthāya śakti patnī pativratā
tataḥ sāsūta tanayaṃ daśame māsi suprabham
śaktipatnī yathā śaktiṃ śaktimantamarundhatī
asūta sā ditirviṣṇuṃ yatā svāhā guhaṃ sutam
agniṃ yathāraṇiḥ patnī śakteḥ sākṣātparāśaram
yadā tadā śaktisūjuravatīrṇo mahītale
śaktistyaktvā tadā duḥkhaṃ pitṝṇāṃ samatāṃ yayau
bhrātṛbhiḥ saha puṇyātmā ādityair iva bhāskaraḥ
rarāja pitṛlokastho vāsiṣṭho munipuṅgavāḥ
jagustadā ca pitaro nanṛtuś ca pitāmahāḥ
prapitāmahāś ca viprendrā hyavatīrṇe parāśare
puṣkarādyāś ca sasṛjuḥ puṣpavarṣaṃ ca khecarāḥ
pureṣu rākṣasānāṃ ca praṇādaṃ viṣamaṃ dvijāḥ
āśramasthāś ca munayaḥ samūhurharṣasaṃtatim
avatīrṇo yathā hyaṇḍādbhānuḥ so 'pi parāśaraḥ
adṛśyantyāścaturvaktro meghajālāddivākaraḥ
sukhaṃ ca duḥkhamabhavadadṛśyantyāstathā dvijāḥ
dṛṣṭvā putraṃ patiṃ smṛtvā arundhatvā munestathā
dṛṣṭvā ca tanayaṃ bālā parāśaramatidyutim
lalāpa vihvalā bālā sannakaṇṭhī papāta ca
sā parāśaramaho mahāmatiṃ devadānavagaṇaiś ca pūjitam
jātamātramanaghaṃ śucismitā budhya sāśrunayanā lalāpa ca
tyajya dīnavadanāṃ vanāntare putra darśanaparāmimāṃ prabho
śakte svaṃ ca sutaṃ paśya bhrātṛbhiḥ saha ṣaṇmukham
yathā maheśvaropaśyatsagaṇo hṛṣitānanaḥ
śrutvā snuṣāmuvācedaṃ mārodīriti duḥkhitaḥ
ājñayā tasya sā śokaṃ visiṣṭhasya kulāṅganā
tyaktvā hyapālayadbālaṃ bālā bālamṛgekṣaṇā
dṛṣṭvā tāmabalāṃ prāha maṅgalābharaṇair vinā
āsīnāmākulāṃ sādhvīṃ bāṣpaparyākule kṣaṇām
śākteya uvāca
aṃba maṅgalavibhūṣaṇair vinā dehayaṣṭiranaghe na śobhate
mātarmātaḥ kathaṃ tyaktvā maṅgalābharaṇāni vai
āsīnā bhartṛhīneva vaktumarhasi śobhane
adṛśyantī tadā vākyaṃ śrutvā tasya sutasya sā
na kiñcidabravītputraṃ śubhaṃ vā yadi vetarat
adṛśyantīṃ punaḥ prāha śākteyo bhagavānmama
mātaḥ kutra mahātejāḥ pitā vada vadeti tām
śrutvā ruroda sā vākyaṃ putrasyātīva vihvalā
śrutvā vasiṣṭho 'pi papāta bhūmau pautrasya vākyaṃ sa rudandayāluḥ
arundhatī cāśramavāsinastadā munervasiṣṭhasya munīśvarāś ca
śrutvā vasiṣṭho 'pi papāta bhūmau pautrasya vākyaṃ sa rudandayāluḥ
arundhatī cāśramavāsinastadā munervasiṣṭhasya munīśvarāś ca
bhakṣito rakṣasā mātuḥ pitā tava mukāditi
parāśara uvāca
kṣaṇena mātaḥ pitaraṃ darśayāmīti me matiḥ
sā niśamya vacanaṃ tadā śubhaṃ sasmitā tanayamāha vismitā
jñātvā śaktisutasyāsya saṃkalpaṃ munipuṅgavaḥ
vasiṣṭho bhagavānprāha pautraṃ dhīmān ghṛṇānidhiḥ
tathāpi śṛṇu lokasya kṣayaṃ kartuṃ na cārhasi
rākṣasānāmabhāvāya kuru sarvesvarārcanam
trailokyaṃ śṛṇu śākteya aparādhyati kiṃ tava
rākṣasānāmabhāvāya matiṃ cakre mahāmatiḥ
adṛśyantīṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ ca praṇamyārundhatīṃ tataḥ
kṛtvaikaliṅgaṃ kṣaṇikaṃ pāṃsunā munisānnidhau
saṃpūjya śivasūktena tryaṃbakena śubhena ca
nīlarudraṃ ca śākteyastathā rudraṃ ca śobhanam
vāmīyaṃ pavamānaṃ ca pañcabrahma tathaiva ca
hotāraṃ liṅgasūktaṃ ca atharvaśira eva ca
aṣṭāṅgamarghyaṃ rudrāya datvābhyarcya yathāvidhi
parāśara uvāca
pitā mama mahātejā bhratṛbhiḥ saha śaṅkara
taṃ dṛṣṭvā bhagavānnudro devīmāha ca śaṅkaraḥ
sā ca dṛṣṭvā mahādevī parāśaramaninditā
prāha bhartāramīśānaṃ śaṅkaraṃ jagatāmumā
bhāryāmāryāmumāṃ prāha tato hālāhalāśanaḥ
rakṣāmyenaṃ dvijaṃ bālaṃ phullendīvaralocanam
dadāmi dṛṣṭiṃ madrūpadarśanakṣama eṣa vai
so 'pi dṛṣṭvā mahādevamānandāsrāvilekṣaṇaḥ
nipapāta ca hṛṣṭātmā pādayostasya sādaram
punarbhavānyāḥ pādau ca nandinaś ca mahātmanaḥ
saphalaṃ jīvitaṃ medya brahmādyāṃstāṃstadāha saḥ
rakṣārthamāgatastvadya mama vālendubhūṣaṇaḥ
pitaraṃ bhrātṛbhiḥ sārdha śāktoyastu parāśaraḥ
bhrātṛbhiḥ sahitaṃ dṛṣṭvā nanāma ca jaharṣa ca
tadā vṛṣadhvajo devaḥ sabhāryaḥ sagaṇeśvaraḥ
śrīdeva uvāca
adṛśyantīṃ ca viprendra vasiṣṭhaṃ pitaraṃ tava
vasiṣṭhaṃ ca tadā śreṣṭhaṃśaktir vai śaṅkarājñayā
mātaraṃ ca mahābhāgāṃ kalyāṇīṃ patidevatām
arundhatīṃ jagannāthaniyogātprāha śaktimān
vāsiṣṭha uvāca
labdhamadyānanaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tava bāla mamājñayā
adṛśyantīṃ mahābhāgāṃ rakṣa vatsa mahāmate
arundhatīṃ ca pitaraṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ mama sarvadā
īpsitaṃ varayeśānaṃ jagatāṃ prabhavaṃ prabhum
gamiṣyāmyabhivandyeśaṃ bhrātṛbhiḥ saha śakaram
nirīkṣya bhāryāṃ sadasi jagāma pitaraṃ vaśī
gataṃ dṛṣṭvātha pitaraṃ tadābhyarcyaiva śaṅkaram
dadāha rākṣasānāṃ tu kulaṃ mantreṇa mantravit
tadāha pautraṃ dharmajño vasiṣṭho munibhir vṛtaḥ
rākṣasā nāparādhyanti pitus te vihitaṃ tathā
yaśasastapasaścaiva krodho nāśakaraḥ smṛtaḥ
vasiṣṭhena tu dattārghyaḥ kṛtāsanaparigrahaḥ
parāśaramuvācedaṃ praṇipatya sthitaṃ muniḥ
prasādādvaiṣṇavaṃ cakre purāṇaṃ vai parāśaraḥ
ṣaṭprakāraṃ samastārthasādhakaṃ jñānasaṃcayam
ṣaṭsāhasramitaṃ sarvaṃ vedārthena ca saṃyutam
caturthaṃ hi purāṇānāṃ saṃhitāsu suśobhanam
eṣa vaḥ kathitaḥ sarve vāsiṣṭhānāṃ samāsataḥ
prabhavaḥ śaktisūnoś ca prabhāvo munipuṅgavāḥ
ṛṣa ūcuḥ
ādityavaṃśaṃ somasya vaṃśaṃ vaṃśavidāṃ vara
sūta uvāca
chāyā ca tasmātsuṣuve sāvarṇi bhāskarāddvijāḥ
yameva tāḍitā sā tu chāyā vai duḥkhitābhavat
pūyaśoṇita saṃpūrṇaṃ kṛmīṇāṃ nicayānvitam
sopi gokarṇamāśritya phalakenānilāśanaḥ
labdhavāndevadevasya prabhāvācchūlapāṇinaḥ
rūpaṃ tvāṣṭrī svadehāttu chāyākhyāṃ sā tvakalpayat
vaḍavārūpamāsthāya tapastepe tu suvratā
kālātprayatnato jñātvā chāyāṃ chāyāpatiḥ prabhuḥ
vaḍavāmagamatsaṃjñāmaśvarūpeṇa bhāskaraḥ
vaḍavā ca tadā tvāṣṭrī saṃjñā tasmāddivākarāt
suṣuve cāśvinau devau devānāṃ tu bhiṣagvarau
likhito bhāskaraḥ paścātsaṃjñāpitrā mahātmanā
nirmame bhagavāṃstvaṣṭā pradhānaṃ divyamāyudham
rudraprasādācca śubhaṃ sudarśanamiti smṛtam
labdhavān bhagavāṃścakraṃ kṛṣṇaḥ kālāgnisannabham
ilā jyeṣṭhā variṣṭhā ca puṃstvaṃ prāpa ca yā purā
sudyumna iti vikhyātā puṃstvaṃ prāptā tvilā purā
mitrāvaruṇayostvatra prasādānmunipuṅgavāḥ
punaḥ śaravaṇaṃ prāpya strītvaṃ prāpto bhavājñayā
sudyumno mānavaḥ śrīmān somavaṃśapravṛddhaye
somavaṃśāgrajo dhīmānbhavabhaktaḥ pratāpavān
ikṣvākorvaṃśavistāraṃ paścādvakṣye tapodhanāḥ
gayā gayasya cākhyātā purī paramaśobhanā
surāṇāṃ saṃsthitiryasyāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca sadā sthitiḥ
tataḥ pṛthurmuniśreṣṭhā viśvakaḥ pārthivas tathā
mucukundaś ca puṇyātmā trayastrailokyaviśrutāḥ
prasādādbrahyasūnorvai taṇḍinaḥ prāpya siṣyatām
dhanahīnaś ca dharmātmā dṛṣṭavān brahmaṇaḥ sutam
taṇḍisaṃjñaṃ dvijaṃ tasmāllabdhavāndvijasattamāḥ
tatastasmānnṛpo labdhvā taṇḍinā kathitaṃ purā
nāmnāṃ sahasraṃ japtvā vai gāṇapatyamavāptavān
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
nāmnāṃ sahasraṃ viprāṇāṃ vaktamarhasi śobhanam
sūta uvāca
yajjaptvā tu muniśreṣṭhā gāṇapatyamavāptavān
saṃvatsaraḥ kṛto mantra prāṇāyāmaḥ parantapaḥ
bahu bhūto bahudhanaḥ sarvasāro 'mṛteśvaraḥ
khaṅgī śaṅkhī jaṭī jvālī khacaro dyucaro balī
mekhalākṛtirūpaś ca jalācāraḥ stuta tathā
vīṇī ca paṇavī tālī nālī kalikaṭus tathā
vṛkṣaḥ śrīmālakarmā ca sarvabandhavimocanaḥ
bhukṣuś ca bhikṣurūpī ca raudrarūpaḥ surūpavān
mano vego niśācāraḥ sarvalokaśubhapradaḥ
nandīśvaraḥ sunandī ca nandano viṣamardanaḥ
bhagahārī niyantā ca kālo lokapitāmahaḥ
itihāsaś ca kalpaś ca damano jagadīśvaraḥ
nīlas tathāṅgaluptaś ca śobhano naravigrahaḥ
asapatnaḥ prasādaś ca pratyayo gītasādhakaḥ
āśramasthaḥ kapotastho viśvakarmā patirvirāṭ
kapilaḥ kalaśaḥ sthūla āyudhaścaiva romaśaḥ
laṃbito laṃbitoṣṭhaś ca laṃbahasto varapradaḥ
krūrakartā krūravāsī tanurātmā mahauṣadhaḥ
prāṇeśo bandhakī vṛkṣo nakulaścādrikas tathā
vasubhṛtsarvabhūtātmā niścalaḥ suvidurbudhaḥ
mahāmanā mahākāmo cittakāmo jitendriyaḥ
gāndhāraś ca surāpaś ca tāpakarmarato hitaḥ
mahāketurdharādhātā naikatānarataḥ svaraḥ
tāraṇaścaraṇo dhātā paridhā paripūjitaḥ
mahākaṇṭho mahāyogī yugo yugakaro hariḥ
girāvāso visargaś ca sarvalakṣaṇalakṣavit
vāryakṣaḥ kakubho vajrī dīptatejāḥ sahasrapāt
tasmāllabdhvā stavaṃ śaṃbhornṛpastrailokyaviśrutaḥ
gaṇādhipatyaṃ saṃprāptastaṇḍinastejasā prabhoḥ
saṃvatsaraṃ kramājjaptvā trisaṃdhyaṃ śaṅkarāśrame
sūta uvāca
sarvalokeṣu vikhyātastriśaṅkuriti vīryavān
vasiṣṭhakopātpuṇyātmā rājā satyavrataḥ purā
viśvāmitro mahātejā varaṃ dattvā triśaṅkave
nābhāgastasya dāyādo bhavabhaktaḥ pratāpavān
rājā daśarathastasmācchrīmānikṣvākuvaṃśakṛt
teṣāṃ śreṣṭho mahātejā rāmaḥ paramavīryavān
bhārate yo mahātejāḥ saubhadreṇa nipātitaḥ
sarve pāśupate jñānamadhītya parameśvaram
samabhyarcya yathājñānamiṣṭvā yajñairyathāvidhi
divaṃ gatā mahātmānaḥ kecinmuktātmayoginaḥ
nṛgo brāhmaṇaśāpena kṛkalāsatvamāgataḥ
ṛtastasya sutaḥ śrīmānsarvadharmavidāṃvaraḥ
kṛtastasya sudharmābhūtpṛṣito nāma viśrutaḥ
karūṣasya tu kārūṣāḥ sarve prakhyātakīrtayaḥ
pṛṣito hiṃsayitvā gāṃ guroḥ prāpa sukalmaṣam
diṣṭaputrāstu nābhāgastasmādapi bhalandanaḥ
bhalandanasya vikraānto rājāsīdajavāhanaḥ
ikṣvākoḥ putrapautrādya elasyātha vadāmi vaḥ
sūta uvāca
ailaḥ purūravā nāma rudrabhaktaḥ pratāpavān
cakre tvakaṇṭakaṃ rājyaṃ deśe puṇyatame dvijāḥ
pratiṣṭhānādhipaḥ śrīmānpratiṣṭhāne pratiṣṭhitaḥ
vijātiśceti ṣaḍime sarve prakhyātakīrtayaḥ
tāvubhau śubhakarmāṇau stutau vidyāviśāradau
bhavabhaktastu puṇyātmā dharma niṣṭhaḥ samañjasaḥ
yajñayājī jitakrodhaḥ sarvabhūtānukaṃpanaḥ
sa lohagandhānnarmukta enasā ca mahāyaśāḥ
yajñasyāvabhṛthe madhye yāto divyo rathaḥ śubhaḥ
tasmādvaṃśātparibhraṣṭo vasoścedipateḥ punaḥ
sūta uvāca
abhyaṣiñcatpuruṃ putraṃ yayātirnāhuṣaḥ prabhuḥ
kṛtopakārastenaiva puruṇā dvijasattamāḥ
yayātiruvāca
śukreṇa me samādiṣṭā devayānyāḥ kṛte jarā
prārthitena punastena jarā saṃcāriṇī kṛtā
prakṛtaya ūcuḥ
sūta uvāca
tābhir eva naraḥ śrīmānnānyathā karmakoṭikṛt
jīryanti dehinaḥ sarve svabhāvādeva nānyathā
sūta uvāca
śūraś ca śūravīraś ca śūrasenasya cānaghāḥ
śūrasenā iti khyātā deśāsteṣāṃ mahātmanām
durjayaḥ kṛṣṇaputrastu babhūvāmitrakarśanaḥ
atha prasūtimicchanvai kuśaṅkuḥ sumahābalaḥ
śaśarbaduṃstu vai rājā anvayādbratamuttamam
śaṃsāṃti tasya putrāṇāmanantakamanuttamam
uśanāstasya tanayaḥ saṃprāpya tu mahīmimām
smṛtaścośanasaḥ putraḥ siteṣurnāma pārthivaḥ
vīraḥ kaṃbalabarhistu marustasyātmajaḥ smṛtaḥ
aśvamedhe tu dharmātmā ṛtvigbhyaḥ pṛthivīṃ dadau
narmadātīramekākī kevalaṃ bhāryayā yutaḥ
daśārhe naidhṛto nāmnā mahārigaṇasūdanaḥ
tasmātkaraṃbhaḥ saṃbhūto devarāto 'bhavattataḥ
ekṣvākīmavahaccāṃśuḥ sattvastasmādajāyata
jyāmaghasya mayā proktā sṛṣṭirvai vistareṇa vaḥ
yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādvāpi nisṛṣṭiñjyāmaghasya tu
sūta uvāca
sāttvataḥ satyasaṃpannaḥ prajajñe caturaḥ sutān
sṛṃjayyāṃ bhajanāccaiva bhrājamānādvijajñire
ayutāyuḥ śātayuś ca balavān harṣakṛtsmṛtaḥ
vadhaṃ prāpto sahāyaś ca siṃhādeva sudāruṇāt
gāndinīṃ nāma kāśyo hi dadau tasmai svakanyakām
varṣatrayaṃ pratidinaṃ gāmekāṃ brāhmaṇāya tu
yadi dadyāstataḥ kukṣernirgamiṣyāmyahaṃ pitaḥ
asyāmutpādayāmāsa tanayāṃstānnibodhata
sumitrasya suto jajñe citrakaś ca mahāyaśāḥ
pauravī bāhlikasutā saṃpūjyāsītsurairapi
āśritaṃ kaṃsabhītyā ca svātmānaṃ śāntatejasam
jāte rāme 'tha nihate ṣaṅgarbhe cātidakṣiṇe
puruṣo bhagavānkṛṣṇo dharmamokṣaphalapradaḥ
tāṃ kanyāṃ jagṛhe rakṣankaṃsātsvasyātmajaṃ tadā
rakṣakaṃ jagatāṃ viṣṇuṃ svecchayā dhṛtavigraham
bhūbhāranigrahārthaṃ ca hyavatīrṇaṃ jagadgurum
mṛtyur eva na saṃdeha iti vāṇī purātanī
tatastāṃ hantumārebhe kaṃsaḥ sollaṅghya cāṃbaram
devakyāḥ sa bhayātkaṃso jaghānaivāṣṭabhaṃ tviti
prabhāvānmunisārdūlāstayā caiva jaḍīkṛtaḥ
nihatā bahavaścānye devabrāhmaṇaghātinaḥ
kṛṣṇaputrāḥ samākhyātāḥ kṛṣṇena sadṛśāḥ sutāḥ
putreṣveteṣu sarveṣu cārudeṣṇādayo hareḥ
viśiṣṭā balavantaś ca raukmiṇeyārisūdanāḥ
kṛṣṇasya tāsu sarvāsu priyā jyeṣṭhā ca rukmiṇī
uṣyatā vāyubhakṣeṇa putrārthaṃ pūjito haraḥ
ete labdhāstu kṛṣṇena śūlapāṇiprasādataḥ
ṛṣiṃ dṛṣṭvā tvaṅgirasaṃ praṇipatya janārdanaḥ
divyaṃ pāśupataṃ yogaṃ labdhavāṃstasya cājñayā
praluptaśmaśrukeśaś ca ghṛtākto muñjamekhalī
dīkṣito bhagavānkṛṣṇastatāpa ca parantapaḥ
tapasā tasya saṃtuṣṭo dadau rudro bahūn varān
sāṃbaṃ jāṃbavatīputraṃ kṛṣṇāya ca mahātmane
tathā jāṃbavatī caiva sāṃbaṃ bhāryā hareḥ sutam
bāṇasya ca tadā tena ccheditaṃ munipuṅgavāḥ
bhujānāṃ caiva sāhasraṃ śāpādrudrasya dhīmataḥ
tathā duṣṭakṣitīśānāṃ līlayaiva raṇājire
saṃhṛtya tatkulaṃ caiva prabhāse 'tiṣṭhadacyutaḥ
kṛṣṇasya dvārakāyāṃ vai jarākle śāpahāriṇaḥ
śāpaṃ piṇḍārake 'rakṣadvaco durvāsasastadā
tyaktvā ca mānuṣaṃ rūpaṃ jarakāstracchalena tu
cauraiścāpahṛtāḥ sarvāstasya māyabalena ca
mahiṣyastasya kṛṣṇasya rukmiṇīpramukhāḥ śubhāḥ
sahāgniṃ viviśuḥ sarvāḥ kṛṣṇenākliṣṭakarmaṇā
revatī ca tathā devī balabhadreṇa dhīmatā
praviṣṭā pāvakaṃ viprāḥ sā ca bhartṛpathaṃ gatā
pretakāryaṃ hareḥ kṛtvā pārthaḥ paramavīryavān
rāmasya ca tathānyeṣāṃ vṛṣṇīnāmapi suvrataḥ
dravyābhāvātsvaṃyaṃ pārtho bhrātṛbhiś ca divaṃ gataḥ
evaṃ saṃkṣepataḥ proktaḥ kṛṣṇasyākliṣṭakarmaṇaḥ
ityetatsomavaṃśānāṃ nṛpāṇāṃ caritaṃ dvijāḥ
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
sūta uvāca
maheśvaro mahādevaḥ prakṛteḥ puruṣasya ca
paratve saṃsthito devaḥ paramātmā munīśvarāḥ
avyaktaṃ ceśvarāttasmādabhavatkāraṇaṃ param
vigrahaḥ sarvabhūtānāmīśvarājñāpracoditam
praśāntaghoramūḍhatvādaviśeṣāstataḥ punaḥ
śarvaścāṇḍakapālastho bhavaścāṃbhasi suvratāḥ
bhīmaścāvanimadhyastho hyahaṅkāre maheśvaraḥ
aṇḍājjajñe sa eveśaḥ puruṣorkasamaprabhaḥ
tasya vāmāṅgajo viṣṇuḥ sarvadevanamaskṛtaḥ
lakṣmyā devyā hyabhūddeva icchayā parameṣṭhinaḥ
dakṣiṇāṅgabhavo brahmā sarasvatyā jagadguruḥ
anupravṛttastu mahāṃstadenāṃ cirastiratvādviṣayaṃ śriyaḥ svayam
brahmā ca bhagavāṃstasmāccaturvaktraḥ prajāpatiḥ
bhagavān bhagavadbhāvānnirmalatvācchivaḥ smṛtaḥ
āpūrya tābhir ayanaṃ kṛtavānātmano yataḥ
te sattvasya ca yogena sṛṣṭāḥ sattvodbhavāḥ smṛtāḥ
te ūrdhvasrotaso jñeyāstuṣṭātmāno budhaiḥ smṛtāḥ
ityeṣa prākṛtaḥ sargo vaikṛto 'navamaḥ smṛtaḥ
devānṛṣīṃś ca mahato gadatastānnabodhata
mahiṣā gavayākṣāś ca plavaṅgāḥ śarabhā vṛkāḥ
siṃhastu saptamasteṣāmāraṇyāḥ paśavaḥ smṛtāḥ
śatarudrāḥ samātmano bhaviṣyantitī yājñikāḥ
evaṃ bhavatu bhadraṃ te yathā te vyāhṛtaṃ vibho
prāguktā tu mahādevī strī saiveha satī hyabhūt
hitāya jagatāṃ devī dakṣeṇārādhitā purā
rudraḥ paśupatiścāsītpurā dagdhaṃ puratrayam
devāś ca paśavaḥ sarve babhūvustasya tejasā
sa yāti brahmaṇo lokaṃ śrāvayedvā dvijottamān
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
samāsādvistarāccaiva sargaḥ proktastvayā śubhaḥ
kathaṃ paśupatiścāsītpuraṃ dagdhuṃ maheśvaraḥ
kathaṃ ca paśavaścāsandevāḥ sabrahmakāḥ prabhoḥ
purasya saṃbhavaḥ sarvo varalābhaḥ purā śrutaḥ
yathā śrutaṃ tathā prāha vyāsādviśvārthasūcakāt
sūta uvāca
trailokyasyāsya śāpāddhimanovākkāyasaṃbhavāt
daityā ūcuḥ
āyasaṃ cābhavadbhumau puraṃ teṣāṃ mahātmanām
mayaś ca balavāṃstatra daityadānavapūjitaḥ
hairaṇye rājate caiva kṛṣṇāyasamaye tathā
ālayaṃ cātmanaḥ kṛtvā tavāste balavāṃstadā
evaṃ babhūvurdaityānāmatidurgāṇi suvratāḥ
purāṇi trīṇī viprendrāstrailokyamiva cāparam
śobhitaṃ tripuraṃ teṣāṃ pṛthakpṛthaganuttamaiḥ
kṛtvāpi sumahatpāpāmapāpaiḥ śaṅkarārcanāt
daityeśvarairmahābhāgaiḥ sadāraiḥ sasutairdvijāḥ
mahādevetaraṃ tyaktvā devaṃ tasyārcanaṃ sthitaiḥ
sarvadā kṣudhitaiścaiva dāvāgnisadṛśekṣaṇaiḥ
nīlādrimerusaṃkāśairnīradopamaniḥsvanaiḥ
mayena rakṣitaiḥ sarvaiḥ śikṣitairyuddhalālasaiḥ
seṃdrā devā dvijaśreṣṭhā drumā dāvāgninā yathā
athaivaṃ te tadā dagdhā devā deveśvaraṃ harim
kiṃ kāryaṃ devakāryeṣu bhagavāniti sa prabhuḥ
tadā sasmāra vai yajñaṃ yajñamūrtirjanārdanaḥ
yajvā yajñabhugīśāno yajvanāṃ phaladaḥ prabhuḥ
devaṃ te puruṣaṃ caiva praṇemustuṣṭuvustadā
śrīviṣṇuruvāca
sūta uvāca
siṃhanādaṃ mahatkṛtvā yajñeśaṃ tuṣṭuvuḥ surāḥ
punaḥ prāha sa sarvāṃstāṃstridaśāṃstridaśeśvaraḥ
hatvā dagdhvā ca bhūtāni bhuktvā cānyāyato 'pi vā
yajedyadi mahādeva mapāpo nātra saṃśayaḥ
apāpā naiva hantavyāḥ pāpā eva na saṃśayaḥ
hantavyāḥ sarvayatnena kathaṃ vadhyāḥ surottamāḥ
asurā durmadāḥ pāpā api devairmahābalaiḥ
kohaṃ brahmāthavā devā daityā devārisūdanāḥ
yaḥ saptaviṃśako nityaḥ parātparataraḥ prabhuḥ
viśvāmareśvaro vandyo viśvādhāro maheśvaraḥ
sa eva sarvadeveśaḥ sarveṣāmapi śaṅkaraḥ
dharmaniṣṭhāś ca te sarve śrautasmārtavidhau sthitāḥ
rudramiṣṭvā yathānyāyaṃ jeṣyāmo daityasattamān
satārakākṣeṇa mayena guptaṃ svasthaṃ ca guptaṃ sphaṭikābhamekam
sūta uvāca
evamuktvā hariśceṣṭvā yajñenopasadā prabhum
upaviṣṭo dadarśātha bhūtasaṃghānsahasraśaḥ
prāha devo hariḥ sākṣātpraṇipatyasthitān prabhuḥ
viṣṇuruvāca
praviśya naṣṭāste sarve śalabhā iva pāvakam
tatastu naṣṭāste sarve bhūtā deveśvarājñayā
tataḥ parājitā devā dhvastavīryāḥ kṣaṇena tu
sendrāḥ saṃgamya deveśamupendraṃ dhiṣṭhitā bhayāt
kiṃ kṛtyamiti saṃtaptaḥ saṃtaptānsendrakānkṣaṇam
dharmādaiśvaryamityeṣā śrutireṣā sanātanī
pūjasā bhogasaṃpattiravaśyaṃ jāyate dvijāḥ
sūta uvāca
śāstraṃ ca śāstā sarveṣāmakarotkāmarūpadhṛk
sarvasaṃmohanaṃ māyī dṛṣṭapratyayasaṃyutam
etatsvāṅgabhavāyaiva puruṣāyopadiśya tu
puratrayavināśāya prāhaunaṃ puruṣaṃ hariḥ
tataḥ praṇamya taṃ māyī māyāśāstraviśāradaḥ
praviśya tatpuraṃ tūrṇaṃ munirmāyāṃ tadākarot
māyayā tasya te daityāḥ puratrayanivāsinaḥ
śraitaṃ smārtaṃ ca saṃtyajya tasya śiṣyāstadābhavan
muniḥ śiṣyaiḥ praśiṣyaiś ca saṃvṛtaḥ sarvataḥ svayam
strīdharmaṃ cākarotstrīṇāṃ duścāraphalasiddhidam
janāsaktā babhūvustā vinindya patidevatāḥ
nāryaścaranti saṃtyajya bhartṝn svairaṃ vṛthādhamāḥ
strīṇāṃ mātā pitā bandhuḥ sakhā mitraṃ ca bāndhavaḥ
bhartā eva na saṃdehastathāpyāsahamāyayā
puraikāmuniśārdūlāḥ sarvadharmān sadā patim
saṃtyajyāpūjayansādhvyo devānanyāñjagadgurūn
narakaṃ ca jagāmānyā tasmādbhartā parā gatiḥ
tathāpi bhartṝn svāṃs tyaktvā babhūvuḥ svairavṛttayaḥ
evaṃ naṣṭe tadā dharme śrautasmārte suśobhane
tapasā prāpya sarvajñaṃ tuṣṭāva puruṣottamaḥ
śrībhagavānuvāca
śāśvatāya hyanantāya avyaktāya ca te namaḥ
sūta uvāca
jajāpa rudraṃ bhagavānkoṭivāraṃ jale sthitaḥ
seṃdrāḥ sasādhyāḥ sayamāḥ sarudrāḥ samarudgaṇāḥ
devā ūcuḥ
trātā netā jagatyasmindvijānāṃ dvijavatsala
sūta uvāca
sūta uvāca
śrutvā prabhostadā vākyaṃ praṇemustuṣṭuvuś ca te
līlāṃbujena cāhatya kalamāha vṛṣadhvajam
mukuṭaiḥ kaṭakaiścaiva kuṇḍalairvalayaiḥ śubhaiḥ
kalpakadrumajaiḥ puṣpaiḥ śobhitairalakaiḥ śubhaiḥ
hārairvārījarāgādimaṇicitraistathāṅgadaiḥ
tilakaiś ca mahādeva paśya putraṃ suśībhanam
vakravṛndaṃ ca paśyeśa vṛndaṃ kāmalakaṃ yathā
netrāṇi ca vibho paśya śubhāni tvaṃ śubhāni ca
añjanāni vicitrāṇi maṅgalārthaṃ ca mātṛbhiḥ
gaṅgādībhīḥ kṛttikādyaiḥ svāhayā ca viśeṣataḥ
sopi līlālaso bālo nanartārtiharaḥ prabhuḥ
trailokyamakhilaṃ tatra nanarteśājñayā kṣaṇam
nāgāś ca nanṛtuḥ sarve devāḥ seṃdrapurogamāḥ
tuṣṭuvurgaṇapāḥ skandaṃ mumodāṃbā ca mātaraḥ
avā patustadā tṛptiṃ nandinā ca gaṇeśvarāḥ
viveśa divyaṃ bhavanaṃ bhavopi yathāṃ budo 'nyāṃbudamaṃbudābhaḥ
tuṣṭuvuś ca mahādevaṃ kiñcidudvignacetasaḥ
bhāgyavantaś ca daityendrā iti cānye sureśvarāḥ
pūjāphalamimaṃ teṣāmityanye neti cāpare
apatanmunayaścānye devāś ca dharaṇītale
dṛṣṭvāpi devadeveśaṃ devānāṃ cāsuradviṣām
procurnamaḥ śivāyeti pūjya cālpataraṃ hṛdi
tataḥ kapardi nandīśo mahādevāpriyo muniḥ
śūlī mālī tathā hālī kuṇḍalī valayī gadī
vṛṣamāruhya suśvetaṃ yayau tasyājñayā tadā
tato vai nandinaṃ dṛṣṭvā gaṇaḥ kuṃbhodaro 'pi saḥ
nandī bhāti mahātejā vṛṣapṛṣṭe vṛṣadhvajaḥ
sagaṇo gaṇasenānīrmeghapṛṣṭhe yathā bhavaḥ
sitātapatraṃ śailāderākāśamiva bhāti tat
tatrāntarbaddhamālā sā muktāphalamayī śubhā
gaṅgākāśānnipatitā bhāti mūrdhni vibhoryathā
atha dṛṣṭvā gaṇādhyakṣaṃ devadundubhayaḥ śubhāḥ
niyogādvajriṇaḥ sarve vinedurmunipuṅgavāḥ
tuṣṭuvuś ca gaṇeśānaṃ vāgbhir iṣṭapradaṃ śubham
yathā devā bhavaṃ dṛṣṭvā prītikaṇṭa kitatvacaḥ
niyogādvajriṇo mūrdhni puṣpavarṣaṃ ca khecarāḥ
vṛṣṭyā tuṣṭastadā reje tuṣṭyā puṣṭyā yathārthayā
nandī bhavaścāndrayā tu snātayā gandhavāriṇā
puṣpairnānāvidhaistatra bhāti pṛṣṭhaṃ vṛṣasya tat
saṃkīrṇaṃ tu divaḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ nakṣatrairiva suvratāḥ
kusumaiḥ saṃvṛto nandī vṛṣapṛṣṭhe rarāja saḥ
divaḥ pṛṣṭhe yathā candro nakṣatrairiva suvratāḥ
taṃ dṛṣṭvā nandinaṃ devāḥ sendropendrās tathāvidham
tu ṣṭuvurgaṇapeśānaṃ devadevamivāparam
devā ūcuḥ
kūṣmāṇḍagaṇanāthāya yogināṃ pataye namaḥ
sarvadāya śaraṇyāya sarvajñāyārtihāriṇe
vajriṇe vajradaṃṣṭrāya vajrivajranivāriṇe
vajrālaṅkṛtadehāya vajriṇārādhitāya te
raktānāṃ bhavapādābje rudralokapradāyine
bhūtānāṃ bhuvane śānāṃ pataye pāpahāriṇe
namaḥ śivāya saumyāya rudrabhaktāya te namaḥ
sūta uvacā
tataḥ prīto gaṇādhyakṣaḥ prāha devāṃśchilātmajaḥ
sūta uvāca
nemyaḥ ṣaḍṛtavaścaiva tayorvai viprapuṅgavāḥ
dharmo virāgo daṇḍosya yajñāṃ daṇḍāśrayāḥ smṛtāḥ
dakṣiṇāḥ saṃdhayastasya lohāḥ pañcāśadagnayaḥ
kṣaddhā ca gatirasyaiva vedāstasya hayāḥ smṛtāḥ
talāḥ kapotāḥ kāpotāḥ sarve talanivāsinaḥ
merureva mahāchatraṃ mandaraḥ pārśvaḍiṇḍimaḥ
iṣurviṣṇurmahātejāḥ śalyaṃ somaḥ śarasya ca
kālāgnistaccharasyaiva sākṣāttīkṣṇaḥ sudāruṇaḥ
evaṃ kṛtvā rathaṃ divyaṃ kārmukaṃ ca śaraṃ tathā
sārathiṃ jagatāṃ caiva brahmāṇaṃ prabhumīśvaram
purāṇyuddiśya khasthāni dānavānāṃ tarasvinām
teṣāṃ bhāvaṃ tato jñātvā devastānidamabravīt
tasmādvai paśavaḥ sarve devāsuranarāḥ prabhoḥ
tato vināyakaḥ sākṣādbālo 'bālaparākramaḥ
apūjitastadā devaiḥ prāha devānnivārayan
śrīvināyaka uvāca
māmapūjya jagatyasmin bhakṣyabhojyādibhiḥ śubhaiḥ
kaḥ pumānsiddhimāpnoti devo vā dānavopi vā
tatastasmin kṣaṇādeva devakārye sureśvarāḥ
vighnaṃ kariṣye deveśa kathaṃ karttuṃ samudyatāḥ
tataḥ sendrāḥ surāḥ sarve bhītāḥ saṃpūjya taṃ prabhum
abruvaṃste gaṇeśānaṃ nirvighnaṃ cāstu naḥ sadā
āliṅgya cāghrāya sutaṃ tadānīmapūjayatsarvasurendramukhyaḥ
saṃpūjya pūjyaṃ saha devasaṃghairvināyakaṃ nāyakamīśvarāṇām
taṃ devadevaṃ surasiddhasaṃghā maheśvaraṃ bhūtagaṇāś ca sarve
gaṇeśvarā nandimukhāstadānīṃ svavāhanairanvayurīśamīśāḥ
agre surāṇāṃ ca gaṇeśvarāṇāṃ tadātha nandī girirājakalpam
vimānamāruhya puraṃ prahartuṃ jagāma mṛtyuṃ bhagavāniveśaḥ
yāntaṃ tadānīṃ tu śilādaputramāruhya nāgendravṛṣāśvavaryān
devāstadānīṃ gaṇapāś ca sarve gaṇā yayuḥ svāyudhacihnahastāḥ
surāsureśaṃ sahasraraśmirbhagavānsutīkṣṇaḥ
rarāja madhye bhagavānsurāṇāṃ vivāhano vārijapatravarṇaḥ
yathā sumeroḥ sikharādhirūḍhaḥ sahasraraśmirbhagavānsutīkṣṇaḥ
sahasranetraḥ prathamaḥ surāṇāṃ gajendramāruhya ca dakṣiṇe 'sya
samantatastuṣṭuvuriṣṭadaṃ te jayeti śakraṃ varapuṣpavṛṣṭyā
tadā hyahalyopapatiṃ sureśaṃ jagatpatiṃ diviṣṭhāḥ
apāṃ patistatheśāno bhavaṃ cānusamāgatāḥ
vṛṣabhendraṃ samāruhya romajaiś ca samāvṛtaḥ
vāyavyāṃ sagaṇaiḥ sārvaṃ sevāñcakre rathasya tu
ṣaṇmukhopi saha siddhacāraṇaiḥ senayā ca girirāja saṃnibhaḥ
devanāthagaṇavṛndasaṃvṛto vāraṇena ca tathāgnisaṃbhavaḥ
vighnaṃ gaṇeśopyasureśvarāṇāṃ kṛtvā surāṇāṃ bhagavānavighnam
vighneśvaro vighnagaṇaiś ca sārdhaṃ taṃ deśamīśānapadaṃ jagāma
kālī tadā kālaniśāprakāśaṃ śūlaṃ kapālābharaṇā kareṇa
prakaṃpayantī ca tadā suredrānmahāsurāsṛṅmadhupānamattā
mattebhagāmī madalolanetrā mattaiḥ piśācaiś ca gaṇaiś ca mattaiḥ
mattebhacarmāṃbaravoṣṭitāṅgī yayau purastācca gaṇeśvarasya
tāṃ siddhagandharvapiśācayakṣavidyādharāhīndrasurendramukhyāḥ
mātaraḥ suravarisūdanāḥ sādaraṃ suragaṇaiḥ surūjitāḥ
durgārūḍhamṛgādhipā duratigā dordaṇḍavṛndaiḥ śivā bibhrāṇāṅkuśaśulapāśaparaśuṃ cakraāsiśaṅkhāyudham
gajairhayaiḥ siṃhavarai rathaiś ca vṛṣairyayuste gaṇarājamukhyāḥ
halaiś ca phaālairmusalairbhuśurṃbhuśuṇḍairgirīndrakūṭairgirisannibhāste
yayuḥ purastāddhi maheśvarasya sureśvarā bhūtagaṇeśvarāś ca
tathendrapadmodbhavaviṣṇumukhyāḥ surā gaṇeśāś ca gaṇeśamīśam
jayeti vāgbhir bhagavantamūcuḥ kirīṭadattāñjalayaḥ samantāt
yamajihvaḥ śatāśvaś ca kaṇṭhanaḥ kaṇṭhapūjanaḥ
muṇḍordhamuṇḍo dīrghaś ca piśācāsyaḥ pinākadhṛk
pippalāyatanaścaiva tathā hyaṅgārakāśanaḥ
śithilaḥ śithilāsyaś ca akṣapādo hyajaḥ kujaḥ
vṛndaśastaṃ samāvṛtya jagmuḥ somaṃ gaṇairvṛtāḥ
dagdhuṃ puratrayaṃ jagmuḥ koṭikoṭigaṇairvṛtāḥ
trayastriṃ śatsurāścaiva trayaś ca triśatās tathā
mātaraḥ sarvalokānāṃ gaṇānāṃ caiva mātaraḥ
bhūtānāṃ mātaraścaiva jagmurdevasya pṛṣṭhataḥ
bhāti madhye gaṇānāṃ ca rathamadhye gaṇeśvaraḥ
nabhasyamalanakṣatre tārāmadhya ivoḍurāṭ
rarāja devī devasya girijā pārśvasaṃsthitā
śubhāvatī tadā devī pārśvasaṃsthā vibhāti sā
cāmarāsaktahastāgrā sā hemāṃbujavarṇikā
bhātīndradhanuṣākāśaṃ meruṇā ca yathā jagat
hiraṇyadhanuṣā saumyaṃ vapuḥ śaṃbhoḥ śaśidyuti
sitātapatraṃ ratnāṃśumiśritaṃ parameṣṭhinaḥ
yathodaye śaśāṅkasya bhātyakhaṇḍaṃ hi maṇḍalam
sadukūlā śive raktā laṃbitā bhāti mālikā
chatrāntā ratnajākāśātpatantīva saridvarā
kimatra dagdhuṃ tripuraṃ pinākī svayaṃ gataścātra gaṇaiś ca sārdham
manvāma nūnaṃ bhagavānpinākī līlārthametatsakalaṃ pravartum
vyavasthitaśceti tathānyathā cedāḍaṃbareṇāsya phalaṃ kimanyat
gaṇairgaṇeśastu rarāja devyā jagadratho merurivāṣṭaśṛṅgaiḥ
atha nirikṣya sureśvaramīśvaraṃ sagaṇamadrisutāsahitaṃ tadā
puṣyayogopi saṃprāpte līlāvaśamumāpatim
sthāne tava mahādeva ceṣṭeyaṃ parameśvara
tathāpi devā dharmiṣṭhāḥ pūrvadevāś ca pāpinaḥ
iṣuṇā bhūtasaṃghaiś ca viṣṇunā ca mayā prabho
dagdhumarhasi śīghraṃ tvaṃ trīṇyetāni purāṇi vai
somaś ca bhagavānviṣṇuḥ kālāgnirvāyureva ca
mumoca bāṇaṃ viprendrā vyākṛṣyākarṇamīśvaraḥ
tatkṣaṇāttripuraṃ dagdhvā tripurāntakaraḥ śaraḥ
devadevaṃ samāsādya namaskṛtvā vyavasthitaḥ
dṛṣṭvā bhītaṃ tadānīkaṃ devānāṃ devapuṅgavaḥ
kiṃ cetyāha tadā devānpraṇemustaṃ samantataḥ
viṣṇunā ca bhavaṃ devaṃ tripurārātimīśvaram
śrīpitāma uvāca
aghorāṣṭakatattvāya dvādaśātmasvarūpiṇe
agnivarṇāyaraudrāya aṃbikārdhaśarīriṇe
dhavalaśyāmaraktānāṃ muktidāyāmarāya ca
jyeṣṭhāya rudrarūpāya somāya varadāya ca
trilokāya tridevāya vaṣaṭkārāya vai namaḥ
aṣṭakṣetrāṣṭarūpāya aṣṭatattvāya te namaḥ
pañcadhā pañcadhā caiva pañcamantraśarīriṇe
catuḥṣaṣṭiprakārāya akārāya namonamaḥ
dvātriṃśattatvarūpāya ukārāya namonamaḥ
aṣṭadhātmasvarūpāya ardhamātrātmane namaḥ
saptalokāya pātālanarakeśāya vai namaḥ
aṣṭakṣetrāṣṭarūpāya parātparatarāya ca
sahasrapādayuktāya śarvāya parameṣṭhine
navātmatattvarūpāya navāṣṭātmātmaśaktaye
punaraṣṭaprakāśāya tathāṣṭāṣṭakamūrtaye
catuḥṣaṣṭyātmatattvāya punaraṣṭavidhāya te
guṇāṣṭakavṛtāyaiva guṇine nirguṇāya te
mūlastāya namastubhyaṃ śāsvatasthānavāsine
nābhimaṇḍalasaṃsthāya hṛdi niḥsvanakāriṇe
candrabibasthitāyaiva śivāya śivarūpiṇe
vahnisomārkarūpāya ṣaṭtriṃśacchaktirūpiṇe
triprakāraṃ stitāyaiva tretāgnimayarūpiṇe
tatpuruṣāya namostu īśānāya namonamaḥ
namastriṃśatprakāśāya śāntātītāya vai namaḥ
ananteśāya sūkṣmāya uttamāya namostu te
namastrimūrtaye tubhyaṃ śrīkaṇṭhāya śikhaṇḍine
anantāsanasaṃsthāya anantāyāntakāriṇe
vimalāya viśālāya vimalāṅgāya te namaḥ
vimalāsanasaṃsthāya vimalārthārtharūpiṇe
yogināṃ hṛdi saṃsthāya sadā nīvāraśūkavat
pratyāhārāya te nityaṃ pratyāhāraratāya te
pratyāhāraratānāṃ ca pratisthānasthitāya ca
dhāraṇāyai namastubhyaṃ dhāraṇābhiratāya te
dhāraṇābhyāsayuktānāṃ purastātsaṃsthitāya ca
samādhānābhigamyāya samādhānāya te namaḥ
samādhānaratānāṃ tu nirvikalpārtharūpiṇe
kastotumicchetkathamīdṛśaṃ tvāṃ steṣyeha tuṣṭāya śivāya tubhyam
ete gaṇāḥ siddhagaṇaiḥ praṇāmaṃ kurvāti deveśa gaṇeśa tubhyam
līlālasenāṃbikayā kṣaṇena dagdhaṃ kileṣuś ca tadātha muktaḥ
kṛto rathaścaiṣuvaraś ca śubhraṃ śarasanaṃ te tripurakṣayāya
anekayatnaiś ca mayātha tubhyaṃ na dṛṣṭaṃ phalaṃ na dṛṣṭaṃ surasiddhasaṃghaiḥ
anantamūrtiḥ kathamīdṛśantvaṃ toṣyaṃ kathamīdṛśaṃ tvām
stūlāya sūkṣmāya susūkṣmasūkṣmasūkṣmāya sūkṣmārthavide vidhātre
sraṣṭre namaḥ sarvasurāsurāṇāṃ bhartre ca hartre jagatāṃ vidhātre
netre surāṇāmasureśvarāṇāṃ dātre praśāstre mama sarvaśāstre
ādyantaśūnyāya ca saṃsthitāya tathā tvaśūnyāya ca liṅgine ca
aliṅgine liṅgamayāya tubhyaṃ ligāya vedādimayāya sākṣāt
rudrāya mūrdhānanikṛntanāya mamādi devasya ca yajñamūrteḥ
vidhvāntabhaṅgaṃ mama kartumīśa dṛṣṭvaiva bhūmau karajāgrakoṭyā
dehīva devaiḥ saha devakāryaṃ kariṣyase nirgumīśa dṛṣṭvaiva bhūmau karajāgrakoṭyā
ekaṃ sthūlaṃ sūkṣmamekaṃ susūkṣmaṃ sūrtāmūrtaṃ mūrtamekaṃ hyamūrtam
ekaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ vāṅmayaṃ caikamīśaṃ dhyeyaṃ caikaṃ tattvamatrādbhutaṃ te
svapne dṛṣṭaṃ yatpadārthaṃ hyalakṣyaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ nūnaṃ bhāti manye na cāpi
tathāpi bhaktyā vilapantamīśa pitāmahaṃ māṃ bhagavankṣamasva
sūta uvāca
ya imaṃ śṛṇuyāddvijottamā bhuvi devaṃ praṇipatya paṭhet
śrutvā ca bhaktyā caturānanena stuto hasañśailasutāṃ nirīkṣya
stavaṃ tadā prāha mahānubhāvaṃ mahābhujo mandaraśṛṅgavāsī
śiva uvāca
sūta uvāca
tataḥ praṇamya deveśaṃ bhagavānpadmasaṃbhavaḥ
śrīpitāmaha uvāca
devānāṃ caiva sarveṣāṃ tvayī sarvārthadeśvara
vāhanatvaṃ taveśāna nityamīhe prasīda me
sāmarthyaṃ ca sadā mahyaṃ bhavantaṃ voḍhumīśvaram
sūta uvāca
tayoḥ śrutvā mahādevo vijñaptiṃ parameśvaraḥ
sārdhaṃ devyā nandinā bhūsaṃghairantardhānaṃ kārayāmāsa śarvaḥ
tatastadā maheśvare gate raṇādgaṇaiḥ saha
sureśvarāḥ suvismitā bhavaṃ praṇamya pārvatīm
yayuś ca duḥkhavarjitāḥ svavāhanairdivaṃ tataḥ
sureśvarā munīśvarā gaṇeśvarāś ca bhāskarāḥ
tripurārerimaṃ puṇyaṃ nirmitaṃ brahmaṇā purā
mānasairvācikaiḥ kāpais tathā vai kāyikaiḥ punaḥ
sthūlaiḥ sūkṣmaiḥ susūkṣmaiś ca mahāpātakasaṃbhavaiḥ
pātakaiś ca dvijaśreṣṭhā upapātakasaṃbhavaiḥ
pāpaiś ca mucyate jantuḥ śrutvādhyāyamimaṃ śubham
sūta uvāca
gate maheśvare deve dagdhvā ca tripuraṃ kṣaṇāt
sadasyāha suredrāṇāṃ bhagavānpadmasaṃbhavaḥ
pitāmaha uvāca
tārakākṣopi ditijaḥ kamalākṣaś ca vīryavān
vidyunmālī ca daityeśaḥ anye cāpi sabāndhavāḥ
tyaktvā devaṃ mahādevaṃ māyayā ca hareḥ prabhoḥ
sarve vinaṣṭāḥ pradhvastāḥ svapuraiḥ purasaṃbhavaiḥ
yāvatpūjā sureśānāṃ tāvadeva sthitiryataḥ
pūjanīyaḥ śivo nityaṃ śraddhayā devapuṅgavaiḥ
tasmātsaṃpūjayelliṅgaṃ ya icchetsiddhimātmanaḥ
pitaro munayaścāpi piśācāḥ kinnarādayaḥ
arcayitvā liṅgamūrti saṃsiddhā nātra saṃśayaḥ
tasmālliṅgaṃ yajennityaṃ yena kenāpi vā surāḥ
viśodhya caiva bhūtāni pañcabhiḥ praṇavaiḥ samam
prāṇāyāmaiḥ samāyuktaiḥ pañcabhiḥ surapuṅgavāḥ
caturbhiḥ praṇavaiścaiva prāṇāyāmaparāyaṇaiḥ
tribhiś ca praṇavairdevāḥ prāṇāyāmaistathāvidhaiḥ
tataścoṅkāramuccārya prāṇāpānau niyamya ca
jñānāmṛtena sarvāṅgānyāpūrya praṇavena ca
guṇatrayaṃ caturdhākhyam ahaṅkāraṃ ca suvratāḥ
karmendriyāṇi saṃśodhya puruṣaṃ yugalaṃ tathā
cidātmānaṃ tanuṃ kṛtvā cāgnirbhasmeti saṃspṛśet
triyāyuṣaṃ trisaṃdhyaṃ ca dhūlayedbhasitena yaḥ
bhavena pāśamokṣārthaṃ kathitaṃ devasattamāḥ
liṅge purā mayā dṛṣṭe viṣṇunā ca mahātmanā
paśavo naiva jāyante varṣamātreṇa devatāḥ
asmābhiḥ sarvakāryāṇāṃ devamabhyarcya yatnataḥ
pratijñā mama viṣṇoś ca divyaiṣā surasattamāḥ
munīnāṃ ca na saṃdehastasmātsaṃpūjayecchivam
ter'cayantu sadā kālaṃ liṅgamūrtiṃ maheśvaram
tadāprabhṛti śakraādyāḥ pūjayāmāsurīśvaram
sūta uvāca
viśvakarmā dadau teṣāṃ niyogādbrahmaṇaḥ prabhoḥ
padmarāgamayaṃ śakro haimaṃ viśravasaḥ sutaḥ
viśvedevās tathā raupyaṃ vasavaḥ kāntikaṃ śubham
ārakūṭamayaṃ vāyuraśvinau pārthivaṃ sadā
mauktikaṃ somarāḍ dhīmāṃs tathā liṅgamanuttamam
anantādyā mahānāgāḥ pravālakamayaṃ śubham
daitya hyoyamayaṃ ligaṃ rākṣasāś ca mahātmanaḥ
trailohikaṃ guhyakāś ca sarvalohamayaṃ gaṇāḥ
cāmuṇḍā saikataṃ sākṣānmātaraś ca dvijottamāḥ
dārujaṃ nairṛtirbhaktyā yamo mārakataṃ śubham
nīlādyāś ca tathā rudrāḥ śuddhaṃ bhasmamayaṃ śubham
lakṣmīvṛkṣamayaṃ lakṣmīrguho vai gomayātmakam
munayo munisārdūlāḥ kuśāgramayamuttamam
vāmādyāḥ puṣpaliṅgaṃ tu gandhaliṅgaṃ manonmanī
ugrā piṣṭamayaṃ sarve mantrā hyājyamayaṃ śubham
vedāḥ sarve dadhimayaṃ piśācāḥ sīsanirmitam
ṣaḍvidhaṃ liṅgamityāhurdravyāṇāṃ ca prabhedataḥ
teṣāṃ bhedāścaturyuktacatvāriṃśaditi smṛtāḥ
tṛtīyaṃ dhātujaṃ liṅgamaṣṭadhā parameṣṭhinaḥ
turīyaṃ dārujaṃ liṅgaṃ tattu ṣoḍaśadhocyate
mṛnmayaṃ pañcamaṃ liṅgaṃ dvidhā bhinnaṃ dvijottamāḥ
ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu kṣaṇikaṃ liṅgaṃ saptadhā parikīrtitam
śrīprada ratnajaṃ liṅgaṃ śailajaṃ sarvasiddhidam
dhātujaṃ dhanadaṃ sākṣāddārujaṃ bhogasiddhidam
bahudhā liṅgabhejāś ca nava caiva samāsataḥ
mūle brahmā tathā madhye viṣṇustribhuvaneśvaraḥ
rudropari mahādevaḥ pramavākhyaḥ sadāśivaḥ
liṅgavedī mahādevī triguṇā trimayāṃbikā
śailajaṃ ratnajaṃ vāpi dhātujaṃ vāpi dārujam
mṛnmayaṃ kṣaṇikaṃ vāpi bhaktyā sthāpya phalaṃ śubham
suredrāṃbhojagarbhāgniyamāṃbupadhaneśvaraiḥ
stūyamānaḥ supuṇyātmā devadundubhiniḥsvanaiḥ
liṅgasthāpanasanmārganihitasvāyatāsinā
āśu brahmāṇḍamudbhidyanirgacchannirviśaṅkayā
śailajaṃ ratnajaṃ vāpi dhātujaṃ vāpi dārujam
mṛnmayaṃ kṣaṇikaṃ tyaktvā sthāpayetsakalaṃ vapuḥ
vidhinā caiva kṛtvā tu skandomāsahitaṃ śubham
kudagokṣīrasaṃkāśaṃ liṅgaṃ yaḥ sthāpayennaraḥ
nṛṇāṃ tanuṃ samāsthāya sthito rudro na saṃśayaḥ
sarveṣāmeva martyānāṃ vibhordivyaṃ vapuḥ śubham
sakalaṃ bhāvanāyogyaṃ yogināmeva niṣkalam
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
sūta uvāca
paramārthavidaḥ kecidūcuḥ praṇavarūpiṇam
vijñānamiti viprendrāḥ śrutvā śrutiśirasyajam
guruprakāśakaṃ jñānamityanye munayo dvijāḥ
dyaurmūrdhā tu vibhostasya khaṃ nābhiḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ
somasūryāgnayo netre diśaḥ śrotraṃ mahātmanaḥ
caraṇau caiva pātālaṃ samudrastasya cāṃbaram
devāstasya bhujāḥ sarve nakṣatrāṇi ca bhūṣaṇam
vattrādvai brāhmaṇāḥ sarve brahmā ca bhagavānprabhuḥ
indropendrau bhujābhyāṃ tu kṣatriyāś ca mahātmanaḥ
vaiśyāścorupradeśāttu śūdrāḥ pādātpinākinaḥ
puṣkarāvartakādyāstu keśāstasya prakīrtitāḥ
vāyavo ghrāṇajāstasya gatiḥ śrautaṃ smṛtis tathā
puṃsāṃ tu puruṣaḥ śrīmān jñānagamyo na cānyathā
viśuddhā vidyayā sarve brahmavidyāvido janāḥ
dharmādharmau japo homo dhyānināṃ saṃnidhiḥ sadā
niṣkalaḥ sakalaśceti sarvaṃ śivamayaṃ tataḥ
vyomaikamapi dṛṣṭaṃ hi śarāvaṃ prati suvratāḥ
pṛthaktvaṃ cāpṛthaktvaṃ ca śaṅkarasyeti cāpare
jantavo divi bhūmā ca sarve vai pāñcabhautikāḥ
tathāpi bahulā dṛṣṭā jātivyaktivibhedataḥ
duḥkhī ca bhegaṃ duḥkhaṃ ca nānubhūtaṃ vicārataḥ
yogināṃ niṣkalo devo jñānināṃ ca jaganmayaḥ
tṛtīyaṃ sakalaṃ caiva nānyatheti dvijottamāḥ
sakalaṃ munayaḥ kecitsadā saṃsāravartinaḥ
evamabhyarcayantyeva sadārāḥ sasutānarāḥ
daśāre dvādaśāre ca ṣoḍaśāre trirasrake
sa svecchayā śivaḥ sākṣāddevyā sārdhaṃ sthitaḥ prabhuḥ
saṃtāraṇārthaṃ ca śivaḥ sadasadvyaktivarjitaḥ
ūcus tathā taṃ ca śivaṃ tathānye saṃsāriṇaṃ vedavido vadanti
bhaktyā ca yogena śubhena yuktā viprāḥ sadā dharmaratā viśiṣṭāḥ
te yānti cainaṃ na ca yogino 'nye tayā ca devyā puruṣaṃ purāṇam
sūta uvāca
pratiṣṭhāyāḥ phalaṃ sarvaṃ sarvalokahitāya vai
aumaṃ kaumāramaiśānaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ brāhmameva ca
prājāpatyaṃ mahātejā janalokaṃ mahas tathā
eendramāsādya caidratvaṃ kṛtvā varṣāyutaṃ punaḥ
merumāsādya devānāṃ bhavaneṣu pramodate
ekapādaṃ caturbāhuṃ trinetraṃ śūlasaṃyutam
sṛṣṭvā sthitaṃ harīṃ vāme dakṣiṇe caturānanam
aṣṭāviṃśatirudrāṇaāṃ koṭiḥ sarvāṅgasuprabham
pañcaviṃśatikaṃ sākṣātpuruṣaṃ hṛdayāttathā
prakṛtiṃ vāmataścaiva buddhiṃ vai buddhideśataḥ
ahaṅkāramahaṅkārāttanmātrāṇi tu tatra vai
indriyāṇīndriyādeva līlayā parameśvaram
pṛthivīṃ pādamūlāttu guhyadeśājjakaṃ tathā
nābideśāttathā vahniṃ hṛdayādbhāskaraṃ tathā
kaṇṭhātsomaṃ tathātmānaṃ bhrūmadhyānmastakāddivam
sṛṣṭaivaṃ saṃsthitaṃ sākṣājjagatsarvaṃ carācaram
sarvajñaṃ sarvagaṃ devaṃ kṛtvā vidyāvidhānataḥ
vṛṣārūḍhaṃ tu yaḥ kuryātsomaṃ somārdhabhūṣaṇam
hayamedhāyutaṃ kṛtvā yatpuṇyaṃ tadāvāpya saḥ
nṛtyādbhir apsaraḥ saṃghaiḥ sarvataḥ sarvaśaubhitaiḥ
nṛtyantaṃ devadeveśaṃ śailajāsahitaṃ prabhum
sahasrabāhuṃ sarvajñaṃ caturbāhumathāpi vā
bhṛgvādyairbhūtasaṃghaiś ca saṃvṛtaṃ parameśvaram
śailajāsahitaṃ sākṣādvṛṣabhadhvajamīśvaram
kṛtvā bhaktyā pratiṣṭhāpya yatphalaṃ tadvadāmyaham
sṛṣṭyantare punaḥ prāpte mānavaṃ padamāpnuyāt
kapālahastaṃ deveśaṃ kṛṣṇakuñcitamūrdhajam
ibhendradārakaṃ devaṃ sāṃbaṃ siddhārthadaṃ prabhum
sudhūmravarṇaṃ raktākṣaṃ trinetraṃ candrabhūṣaṇam
kākapakṣadharaṃ mūrdhnā nāgaṭaṅkadharaṃ haram
siṃhājinottarīyaṃ ca mṛgacarmāṃbaraṃ prabhum
tīkṣṇadaṃṣṭraṃ gadāhastaṃ kapālodyatapāṇinam
huṃphaṭkāre mahāśabdaśabditākhiladiṅmukham
puṇḍarīkājinaṃ dorbhyāṃ bibhrantaṃ kaṃbukaṃ tathā
hasaṃtaṃ ca nadantaṃ ca pibantaṃ kṛṣṇasāgaram
nṛtyantaṃ bhūtasaṃghaiś ca gaṇasaṃghaistvalaṅkṛtam
ācandratārakaṃ jñānaṃ tato labdhvā vimucyate
kṛtvā bhaktyā pratiṣṭhāpya śivalokaṃ sa gacchati
pūrvadevāmarāṇāṃ ca yatsthānaṃ sakalepsitam
tripuṇḍradhāriṇasteṣāṃ śiromālādharasya ca
brahmaṇaḥ keśakenaikamupavītaṃ ca bibhrataḥ
viṣṇoḥ kalevaraṃ caiva bibhrataḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ
kṛtvā bhaktyā pratiṣṭhāpya mucyate bhavasāgarāt
oṃnamo nīlakaṇṭhāya iti puṇyākṣarāṣṭakam
mantramāha sakṛdvā yaḥ pātakaiḥ sa vimucyate
mantreṇānenaṃ gandhādyairbhaktyā vittānusārataḥ
saṃpūjya devadeveśaṃ śivaloke mahīyate
kṛtvā bhaktyā pratiṣṭhāpya dvidhābhūtaṃ jalandharam
sudarśanapradaṃ devaṃ sākṣātpūrvoktalakṣaṇam
tiṣṭhatotha nikuṃbhasya pṛṣṭhataścaraṇāṃbujam
vāmetaraṃ suvinyasya vāme cāliṅgya cādrijām
śūlāgre kūrparaṃ sthāpya kiṅkiṇī kṛtapannagam
saṃprekṣya cāndhakaṃ pārśve kṛtāñjalipuṭaṃ sthitam
dhanurbāṇasamāyuktaṃ somaṃ somārdhabhūṣaṇam
tadākāratayā sopi gatvā śivapuraṃ sukhī
krīḍate nātra saṃdeho dvitīya iva śaṅkaraḥ
gaṅgādharaṃ sukhāsīnaṃ candraśekharameva ca
gaṅgayā sahitaṃ caiva vāmotsaṃgeṃbikānvitam
vināyakaṃ tathā skandaṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ durgāṃ suśobhanām
bhāskaraṃ ca tathā somaṃ brahmāṇīṃ ca maheśvarīm
kaumārīṃ vaiṣṇavaīṃ devaīṃ vārāhīṃ vāradāṃ tathā
indrāṇīṃ caiva cāmuṇḍāṃ vīrabhadrasamanvitām
vighneśena ca yo dhīmān śivasāyujyamāpnuyāt
liṅgamūrtiṃ mahājvālamālāsaṃvṛtamavyayam
vyomnikuryāttathā liṅgaṃ brahmāṇaṃ haṃsarūpiṇam
viṣṇuṃ varāharūpeṇa liṅgasyādhastvadhomukham
kṣetrasaṃrakṣakaṃ devaṃ tathā pāśupataṃ prabhum
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
liṅgapratiṣṭhāpuṇyaṃ ca liṅgasthāpanameva ca
liṅgānāṃ caiva bhedāś ca śrutaṃ tava mukhādiha
sūta uvāca
tathāpi bhaktāḥ parameśvarasya kṛtveṣṭaloṣṭairapi rudralokam
bālyāttu loṣṭena ca kṛtvā mṛdāpi vā pāṃsubhir ādidevam
gṛhaṃ ca tādṛgvidhamasya śaṃbhoḥ saṃpūjya rudratvamavāpnuvanti
kesaraṃ nāgaraṃ vāpi drāvīḍaṃ vā tathāparam
mandaraṃ vā prakurvīta śivāya vidhipūrvakam
jñānayogaṃ samāsādya gāṇapatyaṃ labhennaraḥ
yaḥ kuryānmerunāmānaṃ prāsādaṃ parameṣṭhinaḥ
niṣadhaṃ nāma yaḥ kuryātprāsādaṃ bhaktitaḥ sudhīḥ
kuryādvā yaḥ śubhaṃ viprā himaśailamanuttamam
nīlādriśikharākhyaṃ vā prāsādaṃ yaḥ suśobhanam
mahendraśailanāmānaṃ prāsādaṃ rudrasaṃmatam
jñānaṃ vicāritaṃ rudraiḥ saṃprāpya munipuṅgavāḥ
hemnā yastu prakṛrvīta prāsādaṃ ratnaśobhitam
dravīḍaṃ nāgaraṃ vāpi kesaraṃ vā vidhānataḥ
kūṭaṃ vā maṇḍapaṃ vāpi samaṃ vā dīrgham eva ca
pūrvavatkārayedyastu dvārādyaiḥ suśubhaṃ dvijāḥ
yaḥ sa yāti na saṃdehaḥ svargalokaṃ sabāndhavaḥ
tasmād āyatanaṃ bhaktyā yaḥ kuryān munisattamāḥ
prasādārtha maheśasya prāsāde munipuṅgavāḥ
aśaktaścenmuniśreṣṭhāḥ prāsādaṃ kartumuttamam
yaḥ kuryādvastrapūtena gandhagomayavāriṇā
ālepanaṃ yathānyāyaṃ varṣacāndrāyaṇaṃ labhet
ardhakrośaṃ śivakṣetraṃ śivaliṅgātsamantataḥ
yas tyajed dustyajān prāṇāñ śivasāyujyam āpnuyāt
svāyaṃbhuvasya mānaṃ hi tathā bāṇasya suvratāḥ
svāyaṃbhuve tadardhaṃ syātsyādārṣe ca tadardhakam
mānuṣe ca tadardhaṃ syātkṣetramānaṃ dvijottamāḥ
evaṃ yatīnāmāvāse kṣetramānaṃ dvijottamāḥ
rudrāvatāre cādyaṃ yacchiṣye caiva praśiṣyake
narāvatāre tacciṣye catchiṣye ca praśīṣyake
śrīparvate mahāpuṇye tasya prānte ca vā dvijāḥ
tasminvā yastyajetprāṇāñchivasāyujyamāpnuyāt
vārāṇasyāṃ tathāpyevamavimukte viśeṣataḥ
kedāre ca mahākṣetre prayāge ca viśeṣataḥ
kurukṣetra ca yaḥ prāṇānsaṃtyajedyāti nirvṛtim
prabhāse puṣkare 'vantyāṃ tathā caivāmareśvare
vaṇīśelākule caiva mṛto yāti śivātmatām
vārāṇasyāṃ mṛto janturna jātu jantutāṃ vrajet
śālake vā tyajetprāṇāṃs tathā vai jaṃbukeśvare
śukreśvare vā gokarṇe bhāskareśe guheśvare
sa yāti śivatāṃ yogī mānuṣe devīkepi vā
ārṣe vāpi muniśreṣṭhās tathā svāyaṃbhuvepi vā
ādhāyāgniṃ śivakṣetre saṃpūjya parameśvaram
śivakṣetre muniśreṣṭhāḥ śivasāyujyamāpnuyāt
chittvā pādadvayaṃ cāpi śivakṣetre vasettu yaḥ
kṣetrasya darśanaṃ puṇyaṃ praveśastaccha tādhikaḥ
kṣīrasnānaṃ tato viprāḥ śatādhikamanuttamam
ghṛtasnānena cānantaṃ śārkare tacchatādhikam
śivakṣetrasamīpasthāṃ nadīṃ prāpyāvagāhya ca
śivakṣetrasamīpasthā nadyaḥ sarvāḥ suśobhanāḥ
snātvā teṣu naro bhaktyā tīrtheṣu dvijasattamāḥ
prātaḥ snātvā muniśreṣṭhāḥ śivatīrtheṣu mānavaḥ
madhyāhne śivatīrtheṣu snātvā bhaktyā sakṛnnaraḥ
dvijās triṣavaṇaṃ snātvā śivatīrthe sakṛ naraḥ
purātha sūkaraḥ kaścit śvānaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhayātpathi
prasaṃgādvāramekaṃ tu śivatīrthe 'vagāhya ca
mṛtaḥ svayaṃ dvijaśreṣṭhā gāṇapatyamavāptavān
yaḥ prātardevadeveśaṃ śivaṃ liṅgasvarūpiṇam
sāyāhne sarvayajñānāṃ phalaṃ prāpya vimucyate
mānasairvācikaiḥ pāpaiḥ kāyikaiś ca mahattaraiḥ
tathopapātakaiścaiva pāpaiścaivānupātakaiḥ
saṃkrame devamīśānaṃ dṛṣṭvā liṅgākṛtiṃ prabhum
māsena yatkṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tyaktvā yāti śivaṃ padam
ayane cārdhamāsena dakṣiṇe cottarāyaṇe
viṣuve caiva saṃpūjya prayāti paramāṃ gatim
pradakṣiṇātrayaṃ kuryādyaḥ prāsādaṃ samantataḥ
savyāpasavyanyāyena mṛdugatyā śucirnaraḥ
vācā yastu śivaṃ nityaṃ saṃrauti parameśvaram
padmarāgamayaiścaiva sphaāṭikaiś ca suśobhanaiḥ
ālikhyā kamalaṃ bhadrandaśahastapramāṇataḥ
pañcabhicca tathā ṣaḍbhir aṣṭābhiśceṣṭadaṃ param
punaraṣṭābhir īśānaṃ daśāre daśabhis tathā
punarbāhye ca daśabhiḥ saṃpūjya praṇipatya ca
śālipiṣṭādibhir vāpi padmamālikhya nirdhanaḥ
dvādaśāraṃ tathālikhya maṇḍalaṃ padammuttamam
ratnacūrṇādibhiścūrṇais tathā dvādaśamūrtibhiḥ
grahaiś ca saṃvṛtaṃ vāpi sūryasāyujyamuttamam
evaṃ prākṛtamapyāryāṃ ṣaḍasraṃ parikalpya ca
madhyadeśe ca deveśīṃ prakṛtiṃ brahmarūpiṇīm
dakṣiṇe sattvamūrtiṃ ca vāmataś ca rajoguṇam
agratastu tamomūrtiṃ madhye devīṃ tathāṃbikām
ātmānaṃ cāntarātmānaṃ yugalaṃ buddhimeva ca
evaṃ vaḥ kathitaṃ sarvaṃ prākṛtaṃ maṇḍalaṃ param
ante ca devadevāya dāpayeccūrṇamaṇḍalam
yena kenāpi vā martyaḥ pralipyāyatanāgrataḥ
sargaś ca bhuvanādhīśaḥ śarvavyāpī sadāśivaḥ
śivabrahmāmṛtaṃ grāhyaṃ mokṣasādhana muttamam
sūta uvāca
śivakṣetra muniśreṣṭhā nānyathā siddhiriṣyate
jantubhir miśritā hyāpaḥ sūkṣmābhistānnihatya tu
yatpāpaṃ sakalaṃ cādbhir apūtābhiściraṃ labhet
saṃmārjane tathā nṝṇāṃ mārjane ca viśeṣataḥ
hiṃsā sadā sṛhasthānāṃ tasmāddhiṃsāṃ vivarjayet
kāryamabhyukṣaṇaṃ nityaṃ snapanaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ
vihitāvihitaṃ nāsti yogināṃ brahmavādinām
yatastasmānna hantavyā niṣiddhānāṃ niṣevaṇāt
na hantavyāḥ sadā pūjyāḥ pāpakarmaratā api
striyaḥ sarvā na hantavyāḥ pāpakarmaratā api
na yajñārthaṃ striyo grāhyāḥ sarvaiḥ sarvatra sarvadā
sarvavarṇeṣu viprendrāḥ pāpakarmaratā api
malinā rūpavatyaś ca virupā malināṃbarāḥ
na hantavyāḥ sadā martyaiḥ śivavaccaṅkayā tathā
vedabāhyavratācārāḥ śrautasmārtabahiṣkṛtāḥ
pāṣaṇḍina iti khyātā na saṃbhāṣyā dvijātibhiḥ
na spṛṣṭavyā na draṣṭavyā dṛṣṭvā bhānuṃ samīkṣate
prasaṃgādvāpi yo martyaḥ satāṃ sakṛdaho dvijāḥ
bhavānti duḥkhitāḥ sarve nirdayā munisattamāḥ
putreṣu dāreṣu gṛheṣu nṝṇāṃ bhaktaṃ yathā cittamathādideve
sakṛtprasaṃgādyatitāpasānāṃ teṣāṃ na dūraḥ parameśalokaḥ
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
saṃvatsarasahasraiś ca tapasā pūjya śaṅkaram
na paśyanti surāścāpi kathaṃ devaṃ yajanti te
sūta uvāca
kathitaṃ tathya mevātra yuṣmābhir munipuṅgavāḥ
tathāpi śraddhayā dṛśyaḥ pūjyaḥ saṃbhāṣya eva ca
prasaṃgāccaiva saṃpūjya bhaktihīnairapi dvijāḥ
bhāvānurūpaphalado bhagavāniti kīrtitaḥ
saṃkruddho rākṣasaṃ sthānaṃ prāpnuyānmūḍhadhīrdvijāḥ
abhakṣyabhakṣī saṃpūjya yākṣaṃ prāpnoti durjanaḥ
khyātiśīlas tathā cāndraṃ strīṣu sakto narādhamaḥ
madārtaḥ pūjayan rudraṃ somasthānamavāpnuyāt
brāhmaṃ hi praṇavenaiva vaiṣṇavaṃ cābhinandya ca
saṃśodhya ca śubhaṃ liṅgam amarāsurapūjitam
jalaiḥ pūtaistatā pīṭhe devamāvāhya bhaktitaḥ
dṛṣṭvā devaṃ yathānyāyaṃ praṇipatya ca śaṅkaram
pādyamācamanaṃ cārghyaṃ dattvā rudrāya śaṃbhave
rocanādyaiś ca saṃpūjya divyapuṣpaiś ca pūjayet
nīlotpalaiś ca rājīvairnadyāvartaiś ca mallikaiḥ
caṅkapairjātipuṣpaiścavakulaiḥ karavīrakaiḥ
apāmārgakadaṃbaiś ca bhūṣaṇairapi śobhanaiḥ
dattvā pañcavidhaṃ dhūpaṃ pāyasaṃ ca nivedayet
śuddhānnaṃ caiva mudgānnaṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ ca nivedayet
kevalaṃ cāpi śuddhānnamāḍhakaṃ taṇḍulaṃ pacet
stutvā ca devamīśānaṃ punaḥ saṃpūjya śaṅkaram
sadyojātaṃ japaṃścāpi pañcabhiḥ pūjayecchivam
pūjayedyaḥ śivaṃ rudraṃ śarvaṃ bhavamajaṃ sakṛt
arcitaṃ parameśānaṃ bhavaṃ śarvamumāpatim
pūjitaṃ vā mahādevaṃ pūjyamānamathāpi vā
dṛṣṭvā prayāti vai martyo brahmalokaṃ na saṃśayaḥ
yo dadyāddhṛtadīpaṃ ca sakṛlliṅgasya cāgrataḥ
sa tāṃ gatim avāpnoti svāśramair durlabhāṃ sthirām
dīpavṛkṣaṃ pārthivaṃ vā dāravaṃ vā śivālaye
āyasaṃ tāmrajaṃ vāpi raupyaṃ sauvarṇikaṃ tathā
śivāya dīpaṃ yo dadyādvidhinā vāpi bhaktitaḥ
sūryāyutasamaiḥ ślakṣṇairyānaiḥ śivapuraṃ vrajet
kārtike māsi yo dadyaddhṛtadīpaṃ śivāgrataḥ
saṃpūjyamānaṃ vā paśyedvidhinā parameśvaram
sa yāti brahmaṇo lokaṃ śraddhayā munisāttamāḥ
āvāhanaṃ susānnidhyaṃ sthāpanaṃ pūjanaṃ tathā
saṃproktaṃ rudragāyatryā āsanaṃ praṇavena vai
pañcabhiḥ snapanaṃ proktaṃ rudrādyaiś ca viśeṣataḥ
brahmāṇaṃ dakṣiṇe tasya praṇavena samarcayet
uttare devadeveśaṃ viṣṇuṃ gāyatriyā yajet
vahnau hutvā yathānyāyaṃ pañcabhiḥ praṇavena ca
vyāsena kathitaḥ pūrvaṃ śrutvā rudramukhātsvayam
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
kathaṃ paśupatiṃ dṛṣṭvā paśupāśavimokṣaṇam
sūta uvāca
hitāya sarvadevānāṃ brahmaṇā ca janārdanaḥ
seṃdrāḥ sasādhyāḥ sayamāḥ praṇemur girimuttamam
bhagavān vāsudevo 'sau garuḍād garuḍadhvajaḥ
sakaladuritahītaṃ sarvadaṃ bhogamukhyaṃ muditakuraravṛndaṃ nāditaṃ nāgavṛndaiḥ
madhuraraṇitagītaṃ sānukūlāndhakāraṃ padaracitavanāntaṃ kāntavātāntatoyam
gireḥ pṛṣṭhe paraṃ śārvaṃ kalpitaṃ viśvakarmaṇā
apaśyaṃstatpuraṃ devāḥ seṃdropendrāḥ samāhitāḥ
praṇemurdūrataścaiva prabhāvādeva śūlinaḥ
jagāma kailāsagiriṃ mahātmā meruprabhāge puramādidevaḥ
gaṇairgaṇeśaiś ca girīndrasannibhaṃ mahāpuradvāramajo hariś ca
atha jāṃbūnadamayairbhavanairmaṇibhūṣitaiḥ
vimānairvividhākāraiḥ prākāraiś ca samāvṛtam
dṛṣṭvā śaṃbhoḥ puraṃ bāhyaṃ devaiḥ sabrahmakairhariḥ
harmyaprāsādasaṃbādhaṃ mahāṭṭālasamanvitam
vajravaiḍūryamāṇikyamaṇijālaiḥ samāvṛtam
nṛtyadbhir apsaraḥsaṃghair bhūtasaṃghaiś ca saṃvṛtam
prāsādaśṛṅgeṣvatha pauranāryaḥ sahasraśaḥ puṣpaphalākṣatādyaiḥ
dṛṣṭvā nāryastadā viṣṇuṃ madāghūrṇitalocanāḥ
viśālajaghanāḥ sadyo nanṛturmumudurjaguḥ
kāściddṛṣṭvā hariṃ nāryaḥ kiñcitprahasitānanāḥ
kiñcidvasrastavastrāś ca srastakāñcīguṇājaguḥ
caturthaṃ pañcamaṃ caiva ṣaṣṭhaṃ ca saptamaṃ tathā
aṣṭamaṃ navamaṃ caiva daśamaṃ ca purottamam
atītyāsādya devasya puraṃ śaṃbhoḥ suśobhanam
suvṛttaṃ sutarāṃ śubhraṃ kailāsaśikhare śubhe
sphaāṭikairmaḍapaiḥ śubhrairjāṃbūnadamayais tathā
nānāratnamayaiścaiva digvidikṣu vibhūṣitam
gopurairgopateḥ śaṃbhornānābhūṣaṇabhūṣitaiḥ
prākārairvividhākārairaṣṭāviṃśatibhir vṛtam
upadvārairmahādvārairvidikṣu vividhairdṛḍhaiḥ
grāmyair anyair mahābhāgā mauktikair dṛṣṭimohanaiḥ
candanairvividhākāraiḥ puṣpodyānaiścaśaubhanaiḥ
taḍāgairdirghikābhiś ca hemasopānapaṅktibhiḥ
strīṇāṅgatijitairhaṃsaiḥ sevitābhiḥ samantataḥ
mayūraiścaiva kāraṇḍaiḥ kokilaiścakravākakaiḥ
śobhitābhiś ca vāpībhir divyāmṛtajalais tathā
rudrastrīgamasaṃkīrṇairjalakrīḍāratais tathā
tatraiva dadṛśurdevā vṛndaṃ rudragaṇasya ca
gaṇeśvarāṇāṃ vīrāṇāmapi vṛndaṃ sahasraśaḥ
suvarṇakṛtasopānān vajravaiḍūrya bhūṣitān
sphaāṭikān devadevasya dadṛśuste vimānakān
teṣāṃ śṛṅgeṣu hṛṣṭāś ca nāryaḥ kamalalocanāḥ
kinnaryaḥ kiṃnarāścaiva bhujaṅgāḥ siddhakanyakāḥ
padmakiñjalkasaṃkāśairaṃśukairatiśobhanāḥ
valayairnūpurairhāraiśchatraiścitraistathāṃśukaiḥ
bhūṣitā bhūṣitaiścānyairmāṇḍitā maṇḍanapriyāḥ
dṛṣṭvātha vṛndaṃ surasuṃdarīṇāṃ gaṇeśvarāṇāṃ surasuṃdarīṇām
jagmurgaṇeśasya puraṃ sureśāḥ puradviṣaḥ śakrapurogamāś ca
dṛṣṭvā ca tasthuḥ surasiddhasaṃghāḥ purasya madhye puruhūtapūrvāḥ
taṃ dṛṣṭvā nandinaṃ sarve pramamyāhurgaṇeśvaram
jayeti devāstaṃ dṛṣṭvā sopyāha ca gaṇaiśvaraḥ
saṃprāptāḥ sarvalokesā vaktumarhatha suvratāḥ
paśupāśavimokṣārthaṃ darśayāsmān maheśvaram
purā puratrayaṃ dagdhuṃ paśutvaṃ paribhāṣitam
śaṅkitāś ca vayaṃ tatra paśutvaṃ prati suvrata
dinadvādaśakaṃ vāpi kṛtvā tadbratamuttamam
nandī śilādatanayaḥ sarvabhūtagaṇāgraṇīḥ
praṇemustuṣṭuvuśvaiva prītikaṇṭakitatvacaḥ
viśodhya teṣāṃ devānāṃ paśutvaṃ parameśvaraḥ
upadiśya munīnāṃ ca sahāste cāṃbayā bhavaḥ
paśūnāṃ ca patiryasmātteṣāṃ sākṣāddhi devatāḥ
tasmātpāśupatāḥ proktāstapastepuś ca te punaḥ
tato dvādaśavarṣānte mukta pāśāḥ surottamāḥ
yayuryathāgataṃ sarve brahmaṇā saha viṣṇunā
yaḥ śrāvayecchrucirviprāñchṛṇuyādvā śucirnaraḥ
sa dehabhedamāsādya paśupāśaiḥ pramucyate
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
vrataṃ pāśupataṃ laiṅgaṃ purā devair anuṣṭhitam
vaktumarhasi cāsmākaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ tvayā śrutam
sūta uvāca
purā sanatkumāreṇa pṛṣṭaḥ śailādirādarāt
aviyogakaraṃ puṇyaṃ bhaktānāṃ bhayanāśanam
ṣaḍaṅgasahitān vedānmathitvā tena nirmitam
sarvadānottamaṃ puṇyamaśvamedhāyutādhikam
kṛtvākanīyasaṃ liṅgaṃsnāpya candanavāriṇā
caitramāsādi viprendrāḥ śivaliṅgavrataṃ caret
kṛtvā haimaṃ śubhaṃ padmaṃ karṇikākesarānvitam
karṇikāyāṃ nyaselliṅgaṃ sphaāṭikaṃ pīṭhasaṃyutam
śvetārkakarṇikāraiś ca karavīrairbakairapi
etairanyair yathālābhaṃ gāyatryā tasya suvratāḥ
saṃpūjya caiva gandhādyairdhūpairdīpaiś ca maṅgalaiḥ
nīrājanādyaiścānyaiś ca liṅgamūrti maheśvaram
agaruṃ dakṣiṇe dadyādaghoreṇa dvijottamāḥ
paścime sadyamantreṇa divyāṃ caiva manaḥśilām
puruṣeṇa muniśreṣṭhā haritālaṃ ca pūrvataḥ
sitāgarūdbhavaṃ viprās tathā kṛṣṇāgarūdbhavam
sitāraṃ nāma dhūpaṃ ca dadyadīśāya bhaktitaḥ
mahācarurnivedyaḥ syādāḍhakānnamathāpi vā
etadvāḥ kathitaṃ puṇyaṃ śivaliṅgamahāvratam
sarvamāseṣu sāmānyaṃ viśeṣopi ca kīrtyate
māsi bhādrapade liṅgaṃ padmarāgamayaṃ śubham
āśvine caiva viprendrāḥ gomedakamayaṃ śubham
pravalenaiva kārtikyāṃ tathā vai mārgaśīrṣake
vaiḍūlyanirmitaṃliṅgaṃ puṣparāgeṇa puṣyake
alābhe rājataṃ vāpi ke valaṃ kamalaṃ tu vā
ratnānāmpayalābhe tu hemnā vā rājatena vā
śailaṃ vā dārujaṃ vāpi mṛnmayaṃ vā savedikam
sarvagandhamayaṃ vāpi kṣāṇikaṃ parikalpayet
haimantike mahādevaṃ śrīpatreṇaiva pūjayet
tadardhārdhena vā rudramaṣṭottaraśatena vā
bilvapatre sthitā lakṣmīrdevī lakṣaṇasaṃyutā
nīlotpaleṃbikā sākṣādutpale ṣaṇmukhaḥ svayam
padmāśritā mahādevaḥ sarvadevapatiḥ śivaḥ
nīlotpalaṃ cotpalaṃ ca kamalaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ
sarvavaśyakaraṃ padmaṃ śilā sarvārthasiddhidā
kṛṣṇāgarusamudbhutaṃ sarvapāpanikṛntanam
gugguluprabhṛtīnāṃ caiva dīpānāṃ ca nivedanam
śvetagarūdbhavaṃ caiva tathā kṛṣṇāgarūdbhavam
saumyaṃ sītāridhūpaṃ ca sākṣānnirvāṇasiddhidam
śvetārkakusume sākṣāccaturvattraḥ prajāpatiḥ
karṇikārasya kusume medhā sākṣādvyavasthitā
karavīre gaṇādhyakṣo bake nārāyaṇaḥ svayam
tasmādetairyathālābhaṃ puṣpadhūpādibhiḥ śubhaiḥ
śuddhānnaṃ vāpi mudgānnamāḍhakaṃ cārdhakaṃ tu vā
upahārāṇi puṇyāni nyāyenaivārjitānyapi
nānāvidhāni cārhāṇi prokṣitānyaṃbhasā punaḥ
kṣīrādvai sarvadevānāṃ sthityarthamamṛtaṃ dhruvam
viṣṇunā jiṣṇunā sākṣādanne sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam
bhūtānāmannadānena prītirbavati śaṅkare
upahāre tathā tuṣṭirvyañjane pavanaḥ svayam
pīṭhe vai prakṛtiḥ sākṣānmahadādyairvyavasthitā
saṃvatsarānte godānaṃ vṛṣotsargaṃ viśeṣataḥ
sthāpayed vā śivakṣetre dāpayed brāhmaṇāya vā
ya evaṃ sarvamāseṣu śivaliṅgamahāvratam
kuryādbhaktyā muniśreṣṭhāḥ sa eva tapatāṃ varaḥ
gatvā śivapuraṃ divyaṃ nehāyāti kadācana
varṣamekaṃ caredevaṃ tāṃstānprāpya śivaṃvrajet
devatvaṃ vā pitṛtvaṃ vā devarājatvameva ca
dravyārthī ca nidhiṃ paśyedāyuḥkāmaścirāyuṣam
idaṃ pavitraṃ paramaṃ rahasyaṃ vratottamaṃ viśvasṛjāpi sṛṣṭam
hitāya devāsurasiddhamartyavidyādharāṇāṃ paramaṃ śivena
saṃpūjya pūjyaṃ vidhinaivamīśaṃ praṇamya mūrdhnā saha bhṛtyaputraiḥ
vyapohanaṃ nāma japetstavaṃ ca pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtya śivaṃ prayatnāt
purākṛtaṃ viśvasṛjā stavaṃ ca hitāya devena jagattrayasya
sūta uvāca
nandinaś ca mukhācchrutvā kumāreṇa mahātmanā
vyāsāya kathitaṃ tasmādbahumānena vai mayā
duṣṭāntakāya sarvāya bhavāya paramātmane
padmāsanasthaḥ someśaḥ pāpamāśu vyapohatu
vāmadevaś ca bhagavānpāpamāśu vyapohatu
śivadhyānaikasaṃpannaḥ sa me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
sūkṣmaḥ surāsureśāno viśveśo gaṇapūjitaḥ
śivadhyānaikasaṃpannaḥ sa me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
ekāśro bhagavānīśaḥ śivārcanaparāyaṇaḥ
śivadhyānaikasaṃpannaḥ sa me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
trimūrtir bhagavān īśaḥ śivabhaktiprabodhakaḥ
śivadhyānaikasaṃpannaḥ sa me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
śrīkaṇṭhaḥ śrīpatiḥ śrīmāñśivadhyānarataḥ sadā
śivārcanarataḥ śrīmān sa me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
trailokyanamitā devī solkākārā purātanī
śivārcanarataḥ śrīmān sa me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
trailokyanamiti devī solkākārā purātanī
dākṣāyaṇī mahādevī bhaurī haimavatī śubhā
aparṇāvaradā devī varadānaikatatparā
umā suraharā sākṣātkauśikī vā kapardinī
khaṭvāṅgadhāriṇī divyā karāgratarupallavā
menāyā nandinī devī vārijā vārijekṣaṇā
aṃbāyā vītaśokasya nandinaś ca mahātmanaḥ
śubhāvatyāḥ sakhī śāntā pañcacūḍā varapradā
sṛṣṭyarthaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ prakṛtitvaṃ gatāvyayā
manonmanī mahādevī māyāvī maṇḍanapriyā
kṣobhiṇī mohinī nityaṃ yogināṃ hṛdi saṃsthitā
ekānekasthitā loke indīvaranibhekṣaṇā
saṃstutā jananī teṣāṃ sarvopadravanāśinī
sā me sākṣānmahādevī pāpāmāśu vyapohatu
caṇḍaḥ sarvagaṇeśāno mukhācchaṃbhorvinirgataḥ
śivārcanarataḥ śrīmān sa me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
jāmātā marutāṃ devaḥ sarvabhūtamaheśvaraḥ
sarvagaḥ sarvadṛk śarvaḥ sarveśasadṛśaḥ prabhuḥ
sanārāyaṇakairdevaiḥ seṃdracandradivākaraiḥ
śivārcanarataḥ śrīmānsa me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
merumandārakailāsataṭakūṭaprabhedanaḥ
saptapātālapādaś ca saptadvīporujaṅghakaḥ
saptārṇavāṅkuśaścaiva sarvatīrthodaraḥ śivaḥ
hatāsuramahāvṛkṣo brahmavidyāmahotkaṭaḥ
baddho hṛtpuṇḍarīkākhyestaṃbhe vṛttiṃ nirudhya ca
nāgendravattro yaḥ sākṣādgaṇakoṭiśatairvṛtaḥ
śivadhyānaikasaṃpannaḥ sa me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
bhṛṅgīśaḥ piṅgalākṣosau bhasitāśastu dehuyuk
śivārcanarataḥ śrīmānsa me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
devasenāpatiḥ śrīmānsa me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
ugro bhīmo mahādevaḥ śivārcanarataḥ sadā
etāḥ pāpaṃ vyapohantu mūrtayaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ
kapālīśaś ca vijñeyo rudrā rudrāṃśasaṃbhavāḥ
śivapraṇāmasaṃpannā vyapohantu malaṃ mama
lokaprakāśakaścaiva lokasākṣītrivikramaḥ
ādityaś ca tathā sūryaścāṃsumāṃś ca divākaraḥ
gaganaṃ sparśanaṃ tejo rasaś ca pṛthivī tathā
candraḥ sūryastathātmā ca tanavaḥ śivabhāṣitāḥ
varuṇo vāyusomau ca īśāno bhagavān hariḥ
prāṇaḥ prāṇeśajīveśau mārutaḥ śivabhāṣitāḥ
suṣeṇaḥ śāśvataḥ pṛṣṭaḥ supuṣṭaścamahābalaḥ
ete vai cāraṇāḥ śaṃbhoḥ pūjayātīva bhāvitāḥ
vyapohantu malaṃ sarvaṃ pāpaṃ caiva mayā kṛtam
mantrajño mantravit prājño mantrarāṭ siddhapūjitaḥ
vyapohantu malaṃ sarve siddhāḥ śivapadārcakāḥ
yakṣo yakṣeśa dhanado jṛṃbhako maṇibhadrakaḥ
pūrṇabhadreśvaro mālī śitikuṇḍalireva ca
karkoṭako mahāpadmaḥ śaṅkhapālo mahābalaḥ
śivapraṇāmasaṃpannāḥ śivadehaprabhūṣaṇāḥ
mama pāpaṃ vyapohantu viṣaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamam
vīṇājñaḥ kinnaraścaiva surasenaḥ pramardanaḥ
atiśayaḥ sa prayogī gītajñaścaiva kinnarāḥ
śivapraṇāmasaṃpannā vyopohantu malaṃ mama
vidyādharaś ca vibudho vidyārāśirvidāṃ varaḥ
vibuddho vibudhaḥ śrīmānkṛtajñaś ca mahāyaśāḥ
vāmadevī mahājaṃbhaḥ kālanemirmahābalaḥ
jaṃbhaḥ kuṃbhaś ca māyavī kārtavīryaḥ kṛtañjayaḥ
garutmān khagatiścaiva pakṣirāṭ nāgamardanaḥ
nāgaśatrurhiraṇyāṅgo vainateyaḥ prabhañjanaḥ
nāgāśīrviṣanāśaś ca viṣṇuvāhana eva ca
ete hiraṇyavarṇābhā garuḍā viṣṇuvāhanāḥ
upamanyustathānye ca ṛṣayaḥ śivabhāvitāḥ
lakṣmīś ca dharaṇī caiva gāyatrī ca sarasvatī
durgā uṣā śacī jyeṣṭhā mātaraḥ surapūjitāḥ
sumukhī durmukhī caiva kāmukhī kāmavardhanī
tathānyāḥ sarvalokeṣu divyāścāpsarasas tathā
śivāya tāṇḍavaṃ nityaṃ kurvantyotīva bhāvitāḥ
śukraḥ śanaiścaraścaiva rāhuḥ ketustathaiva ca
vyapohantu bhayaṃ ghoraṃ grahapīḍāṃ śivārcakāḥ
meṣo vṛṣotha mithunas tathā karkaṭakaḥ śubhaḥ
siṃhaś ca kanyā vipulā tulā vai vṛścikas tathā
aśvinī bharaṇī caiva kṛttikā rohiṇī tathā
śrīmanmṛgaśiraścārdrā punarvasupuṣyasārpakāḥ
maghā vai pūrvaphaālgunya uttarāphaālgunī tathā
hastaścitrā tathā svātī viśākhā cānurādhikā
jyeṣṭhā mūlaṃ mahābhāgā pūrvāṣāḍhā tathaiva ca
uttarāṣāḍhikā caiva śravaṇaṃ ca śraviṣṭhikā
śatabhiṣak pūrvabhadrā tathā proṣṭhapadā tathā
mahākarṇaḥ prabhātaś ca mahābhūtapramardanaḥ
śyenajicchivadūtaś ca pramathāḥ prītivardhanāḥ
koṭikoṭiśataiścaiva bhūtānāṃ mātaraḥ sadā
vyapohantu bhayaṃ pāpaṃ mahādevaprasādataḥ
śivadhyānaikasaṃpanno himarāḍaṃbusannibhaḥ
kundendusadṛśākāraḥ kuṃbhakundendubhūṣaṇaḥ
vaḍavānalaśatruryo vaḍavāmukhabhedanaḥ
catuṣpādasamāyuktaḥ kṣīroda iva pāṇḍuraḥ
rudraloke sthito nityaṃ rudraiḥ sārdhaṃ gaṇeśvaraiḥ
vṛṣendro viśvadhṛgdevo viśvasya jagataḥ pitā
gaṅgā mātā jaganmātā rudraloke vyavasthitā
śivabhaktā tu yā nandā sā me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
mātā gavāṃ mahābhāgā sā me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
rudrapūjāratā nityaṃ sā me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
suśīlā śīlasaṃpannā śrīpradā śivabhāvitā
śivaloke sthitā nityaṃ sā me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
vedasāstrārthatattvajñaḥ sarvakāryābhicintakaḥ
samastaguṇasaṃpannaḥ sarvadevaśvarātmajaḥ
jyeṣṭhaḥ sarveśvaraḥ saumyo mahā viṣṇutanuḥ svayam
āryaḥ senāpatiḥ sākṣādgahano makhamardanaḥ
airāvatagajārūḍhaḥ kṛṣṇakuñcitamūrdhajaḥ
bhūtaiḥ pretaiḥ piśācaiś ca kūṣmāṇḍaiś ca samāvṛtaḥ
śivārcanarataḥ sākṣātsa me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
yoginībhīrmahāpāpaṃ vyapohantu samāhitāḥ
rudrasya tanayo raudraḥ śūlāsaktamahākaraḥ
mātṝṇāṃ rakṣako nityaṃ mahāvṛṣabhavāhanaḥ
yajñasya ca śiraśchettā pūṣṇo dantavināśanaḥ
pādāṅguṣṭhena somāṅgapeṣakaḥ prabhusaṃjñakaḥ
sarasvatya mahādevyā nāsikoṣṭhāvakartanaḥ
mahālakṣmīrjaganmātā sā me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
mahāmohā mahābhāgā mahābhūtagaṇairvṛtā
sarvadā sarvagā devī sā me pāpaṃ vyapohatu
siṃhārūḍhā mahādevī pārvatyāstanayāvyayā
viṣṇornidrā mahāmāyā vaiṣṇavī surapūjitā
śivārcanaratā durgā sā me pāpaṃ vyavohatu
brahmāṇḍadhārakā rudrāḥ sarvalokaprapūjitāḥ
satyāś ca mānasāḥ sarve vyapohantu bhayaṃ mama
kūṣmāṇḍakāś ca te pāpaṃ vyapohantu samāhitāḥ
praṇamya śirasā bhūmau pratimāse dvijottamāḥ
yatpuṇyaṃ caiva tīrthānāṃ yajñānāṃ caiva yatphalam
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
vyapohanastavaṃ puṇyaṃ śrutamasmābhir ādarāt
sūta uvāca
tāni vyāsādupaśrutya yuṣmākaṃ pravadāmyaham
varṣamekaṃ tu bhuñjāno naktaṃ yaḥ pūjayecchivam
snāpya rudraṃ mahādevaṃ saṃpūjya vidhipūrvakam
yāvakaṃ caudanaṃ dattvā sakṣīraṃ saghṛtaṃ dvijāḥ
bhījayedbrāhmaṇāñśiṣṭāñjapecchāntiṃ viśeṣataḥ
kṛṣṇaṃ gomithunaṃ dadyātpūjayeccaiva saṃkaram
paurṇamāsyāṃ mahādevaṃ snāpya saṃpūjya śaṅkaram
dadyādgomithunaṃ vāpi tāmrābhaṃ śūlapāṇaye
śālyannaṃ payasā yuktaṃ ghṛtena ca yathāsukham
goṣṭhaśāyī muniśreṣṭhāḥ kṣitau niśi bhavaṃ smaret
paurṇamāsyāṃ śivaṃ snāpya dadyādgomithunaṃ sitam
jyeṣṭhe māse ca deveśaṃ bhavaṃ śarvamumāpatim
saṃpūjya śraddhayā bhaktyā kṛtvā vai naktabhojanam
raktaśālyannamadhvā ca ādbhiḥ pūtaṃ ghṛtādibhiḥ
vīrāsanī niśārdhaṃ ca gavāṃ śuśrūpaṇe rataḥ
snāpya śaktyā yathānyāyaṃ caruṃ dadyāc ca śūline
dhūmraṃ gomithunaṃ dattvā vāyuloke mahīyate
āṣāḍhe māsi cāpyevaṃ naktabhojanatatparaḥ
bhūrikhaṇḍājyasaṃmiśraṃ saktubhiścaiva gorasam
paurṇamāsyāṃ ghṛtādyaistu snāpya pūjya yathāvidhi
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā ca śrotriyān vedapāragān
śrāvaṇeca dvijā māse kṛtvā vai naktabhojanam
paurṇamāsyāṃ ghṛtādyaistu snāpya pūjya yathāvidhi
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā ca śrotriyān vedapāragān
paurṇamāsyāṃ tu deveśaṃ snāpya saṃpūjya śaṅkaram
nīlaskandhaṃ vṛṣaṃ gāṃ ca dattvā bhaktyā yathāvidhi
tataścāśvayuje māsi kṛtvaivaṃ naktabhojanam
gāṃ ca datvā yathānyāya maiśānaṃ lokamāpnuyāt
paurṇamāsyāṃ ca vidhivatsnāpya dattvā caruṃ punaḥ
dattvā gomithunaṃ caiva kāpilaṃ pūrva vaddvijāḥ
mārgasīrṣe ca māsepi kṛtvaivaṃ naktabhojanam
yavānnena yathānyāyamājyakṣīrādibhiḥ samam
paurṇamāsyāṃ ca pūrvoktaṃ kṛtvā śarvāya śaṃbhave
dattvā gomithunaṃ caiva pāṇḍuraṃ vidhipūrvakam
kuryādvarṣaṃ krameṇaiva vyutkrameṇāpi vā dvijāḥ
sa yāti śivasāyujyaṃ jñānayogamavāpnuyāt
sūta uvāca
naranāryādijantūnāṃ hitāya munisattamāḥ
rathādyairvāpi devaśaṃ nītvā rudrālayaṃ prati
sa yāti śivāsāyujyaṃ nārī devyā yadi prabho
varṣamekaṃ na bhuñjati kanyā vā vidhavāpi vā
pratiṣṭhāpya yathānyāyaṃ dattvā rudrālaye punaḥ
yā nāryevaṃ caredabdaṃ kṛṣṇāmekāṃ caturdaśīm
varṣānte pratimāṃ kṛtvā yena kenāpi vā dvijāḥ
snāpyeśānaṃ yajedbhaktyā sahasraiḥ kamalaiḥ sitaiḥ
rājataṃ kamalaṃ caiva jāṃbūnadasukarṇikam
dattvā bhavāya viprebhyaḥ pradadyād dakṣiṇām api
sāyujyaṃ caivamāpnoti bhavānyā dvijasattamāḥ
kuryādyadvā naraḥ sopi rudrasāyujyamāpnuyāt
niyogādeva tatkāryaṃ bhartṝṇāṃ dvijasattamāḥ
japaṃ dānaṃ tapaḥ sarvamasvatantrā yataḥ striyaḥ
sā bhavānyāś ca sāyujyaṃ sārūyaṃ cāpi suvratā
kārtikyāṃ vā tu yā nārī ekabhaktena vartate
dadyātkṛṣṇatilānāṃ ca bhāramekamatandriyā
saghṛtaṃ saguḍaṃ caiva odanaṃ parameṣṭhine
bhavānyā modate sārdhaṃ sārūpyaṃ prāpya suvratā
mārgaśīrṣakamāsādi kārtikyāntaṃ yatākramam
vrataṃ suvipulaṃ puṇyaṃ nandinā paribhāṣitam
mārgaśīrṣakamāsetha vṛṣaṃ pūrṇāṅgamuttamam
sā ca sārdhaṃ bhavānyā vai modate nātra saṃśayaḥ
puṣyamāse tu vai śūlaṃ pratiṣṭhāpya nivedayet
dadyātsaṃpūjya deveśaṃ brāhmaṇāṃścaiva bhojayet
phaālgune pratimāṃ kṛtvā hiraṇyena yathāvidhi
caitre bhavaṃ kumāraṃ ca bhavānīṃ ca yathāvidhi
bhavānyā modate sārdhaṃ dattvā rudrāya śaṃbhave
kṛtvālayaṃ hi kauberaṃ rājataṃ rajatena vai
īśvaromāsamāyuktaṃ gaṇeśaiś ca samantataḥ
jyeṣṭhe māsi mahādevaṃ liṅgamūrtimumāpatim
haṃsena ca varāheṇa kṛtvā tāmrādibhiḥ śubhām
śivāya śivamāsādya śivasthāne yathāvidhi
brāhmaṇaiḥ sahitāṃ sthāpya devyāḥ sāyujyamāpnuyāt
āṣāḍhe ca śubhe māse gṛhaṃ kṛtvā suśobhanam
dāsīdāsādibhiścaiva śayanairaśanādibhiḥ
saṃpūrṇaiś ca gṛhaṃ vastrairācchādya ca samantataḥ
devaṃ ghṛtādibhiḥ snāpya mahādevamumāpatim
prathamāśramiṇaṃ bhaktyā saṃpūjya ca yathāvidhi
kanyāṃ sumadhyamāṃ yāvat kālajīvanasaṃyutām
bhavānyā sadṛśībhūtvā sarvakalpeṣu sāvyayā
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā ca dāpayecca yathāvidhi
sā ca sūryāṃśusaṃkāśā bhavānyā saha modate
suvarṇavastrayaṃyuktaṃ dattvā saṃpūjya śaṅkaram
sarvadhānyasamāyuktaṃ sarvabījarasādibhiḥ
dakṣiṇe ca yathānyāyaṃ brahmāṇaṃ ca caturmukham
indrādilokapālāṃś ca kṛtvā bhaktyā yathāvidhi
vāme pāśaṃ bhavānyāś ca kamalaṃ hemabhūṣitam
viṣṇoś ca śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ ca gadāmabjaṃ prayatnataḥ
brahmaṇaścākṣasūtraṃ ca kamaṇḍalumanuttamam
yamasya daṇḍaṃ nirṛteḥ khaḍgaṃ niśicarasya tu
varuṇasya mahāpāśaṃ nāgākhyaṃ rudramadbhutam
vāyoryāṣṭiṃ kuberasya gadāṃ lokaprapūjitām
ṭaṅkaṃ ceśānadevasya nivedyaivaṃ krameṇa ca
mahāmeruvrataṃ kṛtvā mahādevāya dāpayet
kārtikyāmapi yā nārī kṛtvā devīmumāṃ śubhām
hematāmrādibhiścaiva pratiṣṭhāpya vidhānataḥ
lokapālais tathā siddhaiḥ saṃvṛtaṃ sthāpya yatnataḥ
mārgaśīrṣakamāsādikārtikāntaṃ pravartitam
naranāryādijantūnāṃ hitāya munisattamāḥ
nārī devyā na saṃdehaḥ śivena paribhāṣitam
sūta uvāca
japādeva na saṃdeho vratānāṃ vai viśeṣataḥ
samāptirnānyathā tasmājjapetpañcākṣarīṃ śubhām
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
kathaṃ pañcākṣarī vidyā prabhāvo vā kathaṃ vada
sūta uvāca
pārvatyāḥ kathitaṃ puṇyaṃ pravadāmi samāsataḥ
śrīdevyuvāca
śrībhagavānuvāca
vācyaḥ pañcākṣarairdevi śivastrailokyapūjitaḥ
vācakaḥ paramo mantrastasya pañcākṣaraḥ sthitaḥ
jñātvā prayogaṃ vidhinā ca siddhiṃ labdhvā tathā pañcamukho mahātmā
tamārādhayituṃ devaṃ parātparataraṃ śivam
tatastutoṣa bhagavān trimūrtīnāṃ paraḥ śivaḥ
tepi labdhvā varānviprāstadārādhanakāṅkṣiṇaḥ
tiṣṭhantonugrahārthāya devi te ṛṣayaḥ purā
pañcākṣaramṛṣicchandau daivataṃ śaktibījavat
tacchrutvā mantramāhātmyamṛṣayaste tapodhanāḥ
mantrasya viniyogaṃ ca kṛtvā sarvamanuṣṭhitāḥ
varṇānvarṇavibhāgāṃś ca sarvadharmāṃś ca śobhanān
yasyaivaṃ hṛdi saṃsthoyaṃ mantraḥ syātpārameśvaraḥ
pañcākṣaraiḥ sapraṇavo mantroyaṃ hṛdayaṃ mama
guhyādguhyataraṃ sākṣānmokṣajñānamanuktamam
bījaṃ śaktiṃ svaraṃ varṇaṃ sthānaṃ caivākṣaraṃ prati
nakārādīni bījāni pañcabhūtātmakāni ca
tvadīyaṃ praṇavaṃ kiñcinmadīyaṃ praṇavaṃ tathā
tvadīyaṃ deviṃ mantrāṇāṃ śaktibhūtaṃ na saṃśayaḥ
akārokāramakārā madīye praṇave sthitāḥ
ukāraṃ ca makāraṃ ca akāraṃ ca krameṇa vai
oṅkārasya svarodātta ṛṣirbrahma sitaṃ vapuḥ
nakāraḥ pītavarṇaś ca sthānaṃ pūrvamukhaṃ smṛtam
indrodhidaivataṃ chando gāyatrī gautamo ṛṣiḥ
makāraḥ kṛṣmavarṇosya sthānaṃ vai dākṣiṇāmukham
chandonuṣṭup ṛṣiścātrī rudro daivatamucyate
śikāro dhūmravarṇosya sthānaṃ vai paścimaṃ mukham
viśvāmitra ṛṣistriṣṭup chando viṣṇustu daivatam
brahmādhidaivataṃ chando bṛhatī cāṅgirā ṛṣiḥ
yakāro raktavarṇaś ca sthānum ūrdhvaṃ mukhaṃ virāṭ
utpattirbrahmacārīṇāṃ gṛhasthānāṃ sthitiḥ sadā
yatīnāṃ saṃhṛtir nyāsaḥ siddhir bhavati nānyathā
aṅganyāsaḥ karanyāso dehanyāsa iti tridhā
aṅganyāsaṃ tataḥ paścādakṣarāṇāṃ vidhikramāt
pādādimūrdhaparyantaṃ saṃhāro bhavati priye
hṛdayāsyagalanyāsaḥ sthitinyāsa udāhṛtaḥ
saśiraskaṃ tato dehaṃ sarvamantreṇa saṃspṛśet
atīva bhogado devī sthitinyāsaḥ kuṭuṃbinām
karanyāsaṃ purā kṛttvā dehanyāsamanaṃ taram
aṅganyāsaṃ nyasetpaścādeṣa sādhāraṇo vidhiḥ
oṅkāraṃ saṃpuṭīkṛtya sarvāṅgeṣu ca vinyaset
karayorubhayoścaiva daśāgrāṃ guliṣu kramāt
smaretpūrvamṛṣiñchando daivataṃ bījameva ca
śaktiṃ ca paramātmānaṃ guruṃ caiva varānane
mantreṇa pāṇī saṃmṛjya talayoḥ praṇavaṃ nyaset
aṅgulīnāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ tathā cādyantaparvasu
ubhābhyāmeva pāṇibhyāmāpādatalamastakam
mantreṇa saṃspṛśeddehaṃ praṇavenaiva saṃpuṭam
kṛtvāṅganyāsamevaṃ hi mukhāni parikalpayet
ṣaḍaṅgāni nyasetpaścādyathāsthānaṃ ca śobhanam
praṇavaṃ hṛdayaṃ vidyān nakāraḥ śira ucyate
śikhā makāra ākhyātaḥ śikāraḥ kavacaṃ tathā
vākāro netramastraṃ tu yakāraḥ parikīrtitaḥ
vighneśo mātaro durgā kṣetrajño devatā diśaḥ
aṅguṣṭhatarjanyagrābhyāṃ saṃsthāpya sumukhaṃ śubham
gale madhye tathāṅguṣṭhe tarjanyādyāṅgulīṣu ca
evaṃ vinyasya medhāvī śuddhakāyo dṛḍhavrataḥ
japetpañcākṣaraṃ mantraṃ labdhvācāryaprasādataḥ
evaṃ ca dakṣiṇāsiddhaṃ mantraṃ siddhaṃ yatastataḥ
śuśrūṣumanahaṅkāramupavāsakṛśaṃ śucim
puraścaraṇajāpī vā api vā nityajāpakaḥ
āsanaṃ ruciraṃ baddhvā maunī caikāgramānasaḥ
ādyāntayorjapasyāpi kuryādvai prāṇasaṃyamān
tathā cānte japedbījaṃ śatamaṣṭottaraṃ śubham
pañcākṣarasya mantrasya prāṇāyāma udāhṛtaḥ
indriyāṇāṃ vaśitvaṃ ca tasmātprāṇāṃś ca saṃyamet
nadyāṃ śatasahasraṃ tu anantaḥ śivasannidhau
aṅgulījapasaṃkhyānamekamekaṃ śubhānane
pañcaviṃśati mokṣārthaṃ saptaviṃśati pauṣṭikam
triṃśacca dhanasaṃpattyai pañcāśaccābhicārikam
śṛṇuṣva sarvayajñebhyo japayajño viśiṣyate
jāpinaṃ nopasarpanti bhayabhītāḥ samantataḥ
evaṃ labdhvā śivaṃ jñānaṃ jñātvā japavidhikramam
yasmādācārahīnasya sādhanaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet
sadācāravatāṃ puṃsāṃ sarvatrāpyabhayaṃ bhavet
tadvadācārahīnānāṃ sarvatraivabhayaṃ bhavet
sadācāreṇa devatvamṛṣitvaṃ ca varānane
durvṛtto śuddhibhūyiṣṭho pāpīyān jñānadūṣakaḥ
udayāstamayātpūrvamāramya vidhinā śuciḥ
kāmānmohādbhayāllobhātsaṃdhyāṃ nātikrameddvijaḥ
saṃdhyātikramaṇādvipro brāhmaṇyātpatate yataḥ
anṛtaṃ paruṣaṃ śāṭhyaṃ paiśunyaṃ pāpahetukam
kvaciccāpi na kurvīta vācā ca manasā tathā
śūdrānnaṃ yātayāmānnaṃ naivedyaṃ śrāddhameva ca
asnātvā na ca bhuñjīyādajapognimapūjya ca
parṇapṛṣṭhe na bhuñjīyādrātrau dīpaṃ vinā tathā
bhinnabhāṇḍe ca rathyāyāṃ patitānāṃ ca saṃnidhau
śūdraśeṣaṃ na bhuñjīyātsahānnaṃ śisukairapi
śuddhānnaṃ snigdhamaśrīyātsaṃskṛtaṃ cābhimantritam
bhoktā śiva iti smṛtvā maunī caikāgramānasaḥ
āsyena na pibettoyaṃ tiṣṭhannañjalināpi vā
vāmahastena śayyāyāṃ tathaivānyaṃkareṇa vā
vibhītakārkakārañjasnuhicchāyāṃ na cāśrayet
staṃbhadīpamanuṣyāṇāmanyeṣāṃ praṇināṃ tathā
nāvaroheta kūpādiṃ nāroheduccapādapān
sūryāgnijaladevānāṃ gurūṇāṃ vimukhaḥ śubhe
na kuryādiha kāryāṇi japakarma śubhāni vā
agnau na tāpayetpādau hastaṃ padbhyāṃ na saṃspṛśet
na jalaṃ tāḍayetpadbhyāṃ nāṃbhasyaṅgamalaṃ tyajet
malaṃ prakṣālayettī re prakṣālya snānamācaret
aśrīkaraṃ manuṣyāṇāmasuddhaṃ saṃspṛśedyadi
ajāśvānakharoṣṭrāṇāṃ mārjanāttupareṇukān
saspṛśīdyādi mūḍhātmā śriyaṃ hanti harerapi
sphigvātaṃ śūrpavātaṃ ca vātaṃ prāṇamukhānilam
sukṛtāni harantyete saṃspṛṣṭāḥ puruṣasya tu
śvanīcadarśanaṃ nidrā pralāpāste japadviṣaḥ
eteṣāṃ saṃbhave vāpi kuryātsūryādidarśanam
ācamya vā japec cheṣaṃ kṛtvā vā prāṇasaṃyamam
ete jyotīṃṣi proktāni vidvadbhir bāhmaṇais tathā
anāsanaḥ śayāno vā rathyāyāṃ śūdrasannidhau
raktabhūmyāṃ ca khaṭvāyāṃ na japejjāpakas tathā
kauśeyaṃ vyāghracarmaṃ vā cailaṃ taulamathāpi vā
dāravaṃ tālaparṇaṃ vā āsanaṃ parikalpayet
trisaṃdhyaṃ tu guroḥ pūjā kartavyā hitamicchatā
saguṇo nirguṇo vāpi tasyājñāṃ śirasā vahet
yathā prajvalito vahnirviṣṭhā kāṣṭhaṃ ca nirdahet
gurustuṣṭo dahatyevaṃ pāpaṃ tanmantratejasā
kurvantyanugrahaṃ tuṣṭā gurau tuṣṭe na saṃśayaḥ
evamācāravān bhakto nityaṃ japaparāyaṇaḥ
yasya yena viyuñjīta kāryeṇa tu viśeṣataḥ
rājyaiśvaryaṃ ca vijñānaṃ svargo nirvāṇa eva ca
prokṣaṇaṃ cābhiṣekaṃ ca aghamarṣaṇameva ca
snāne ca saṃdhyayoścaiva kuryādekādaśena vai
dūrvāṅkurāstilā vāṇī guḍūcī ghuṭikā tathā
teṣāṃ tu daśasāhasraṃ homamāyuṣyavardhanam
aśvatthavṛkṣamāśritya japellakṣadvayaṃ sudhīḥ
śanaiścaradine spṛṣṭvā dīrghayuṣyaṃ labhennaraḥ
śanaiścaradine 'śvatthaṃ pāṇibhyāṃ saṃspṛśetsudhīḥ
samastavyādhiśāntyarthaṃ palāśasamidairnaraḥ
ekādaśena bhuñjīyādannaṃ caivābhimantritam
bhakṣyaṃ cānyattathā peyaṃ viṣamapyamṛtaṃ bhavet
japel lakṣaṃ tu pūrvāhṇe hutvā cāṣṭaśatena vai
nadītoyena saṃpūrṇaṃ ghaṭaṃ saṃspṛśya śobhanam
japtvāyutaṃ ca tatsnānādrogāṇāṃ bheṣajaṃ bhavet
aṣṭāviṃśajjapitvānnamaśrīyādanvahaṃ śuciḥ
hutvā ca tāvatpālaśairevaṃ vārogyam aśnute
candrasūryagrahe pūrvam upoṣya vidhinā śuciḥ
yāvadgrahaṇamokṣaṃ tu tāvannadyāṃ samāhitaḥ
aṣṭottarasahasreṇa pibedbrāhmīrasaṃ dvijāḥ
ehikāṃ labhate medhāṃ sarvaśāstradharāṃ śubhām
sārasvatī bhaveddevī tasya vāgatimānuṣī
grahanakṣatrapīḍāsu japedbhaktyāyutaṃ naraḥ
duḥsvapnadarśane snātvā japedvai cāyutaṃ naraḥ
ghṛte nāṣṭaśataṃ hutvā sadyaḥ śāntirbhaviṣyati
gajānāṃ turagāṇāṃ tu gojātīnāṃ viśeṣataḥ
vyādhyāgame śucirbhūtvā juhuyātsamidhāhutim
utpāte śatrubādhāyāṃ juhuyādayutaṃ śuciḥ
pālāśasamidhairdevi tasya śāntirbhaviṣyati
ābhicārikabādhāyāmetaddevi samācaret
pratyag bhavati tacchaktiḥ śatroḥ pīḍā bhaviṣyati
vidveṣaṇārthaṃ juhuyādvaibhītasamidhāṣṭakam
viṣeṇa rudhirābhyakto vidveṣāṇakaraṃ nṛṇām
prayāścittaṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvapāpāviśuddhaye
pāpaśuddhirna cetpuṃsaḥ kriyāḥ sarvāścaniṣphalāḥ
jñānaṃ ca hīyate tasmātkartavyaṃ pāpaśodhanam
vidyālakṣmīviśuddhyarthaṃ māṃ dhyātvāñjalinā śubhe
śivenaikā daśenādbhir abhiṣiñcetsamantataḥ
aṣṭottaraśatenaiva snāyātpāpaviśuddhaye
saṃdhyopāsanavicchede japedaṣṭaśataṃ naraḥ
spṛṣṭamannaṃ na bhuñjīta bhuktvā cāṣṭaśataṃ japet
upapātakaduṣṭānāṃ tadardhaṃ parikīrtitam
śeṣāṇāmapi pāpānāṃ japetpañcasahasrakam
ātmabodhaparaṃ guhyaṃ śivabodhaprakāśakam
japecca pañcalakṣaṃ tu vigṛhītendriyaḥ śuciḥ
pañcendriyāṇāṃ vijayo bhaviṣyati varānane
bhūtānāmiha pañcānāṃ vijayaṃ manujo labhet
samyagvijayamāpnoti karaṇānāṃ varānane
pañcaviṃśatilakṣāṇāṃ japena kamalānane
pañcaviṃśatitattvānāṃ vijayaṃ manujo labhet
madhyarātre ca śivayoḥ paśyatyeva na saṃśayaḥ
andhakāravināśaś ca dīpasyeva prakāśanam
sabījasaṃpuṭaṃ mantraṃ śatalakṣaṃ japecchuciḥ
śravayecca dvijāñchuddhānpañcākṣaravidhikramam
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
japācchreṣṭhātamaṃ prāhurbrāhmaṇā dagdhakilbiṣāḥ
viraktānāṃ prabuddhānāṃ dhyānayajñaṃ suśobhanam
tasmādvadasva sūtādya dhyānayajñamaśeṣataḥ
vistārātsarvayatnena viraktānāṃ mahātmanām
rudreṇa kathitaṃ prāha guhāṃ prāpya mahātmanām
saṃhṛtya kālakūṭākhyaṃ viṣaṃ vai viśvakarmaṇā
sūta uvāca
guhāṃ prāpya sukhāsīnaṃ bhavānyā saha śaṅkaram
munayaḥ saṃśitātmānaḥ preṇamustaṃ guhāśrayam
astuvaṃś ca tataḥ sarve nīlakaṇṭhamumāpatim
atyugraṃ kālakūṭākhyaṃ saṃhṛtaṃ bhagavaṃstvayā
ataḥ pratiṣṭhitaṃ sarvaṃ tvayā deva vṛṣadhvaja
kimanena dvijaśreṣṭhā viṣaṃ vakṣye sudāruṇam
na viṣaṃ kālakūṭākhyaṃ saṃsāro viṣamucyate
saṃsāro dvividhaḥ proktaḥ svādhikārānurūpataḥ
puṃsāṃ saṃmūḍhacittānāmasaṃkṣīṇaḥ sudāruṇaḥ
īṣaṇārāgadoṣema sargo jñānena suvratāḥ
asannikṛṣṭe tvarthepi śāstraṃ tacchravaṇātsatām
buddhimutpādayatyeva saṃsāre viduṣāṃ dvijāḥ
tasmāddṛṣṭānuśravikaṃ duṣṭamityubhayātmakam
śāstramityucyate 'bhāgaṃ śruteḥ karmasu taddvijāḥ
mūrdhānaṃ brahmaṇaḥ sāramṛṣīṇāṃ karmaṇaḥ phalam
nanu svabhāvaḥ sarveṣāṃ kāmo dṛṣṭo na cānyathā
śrutuḥ pravartikā teṣāmiti karmaṇyatādvidaḥ
nivṛttilakṣaṇo dharmaḥ samarthānā mihocyate
kalā saṃśoṣamāyāti karmaṇānyasvabhāvataḥ
nārakī pāpakṛtsvargī puṇyakṛtpuṇyagairavāt
vyatimiśreṇa vai jīvaścaturdhā saṃvyavasthitaḥ
evaṃ vyavasthito dehī karmaṇājño hyanirvṛtaḥ
ṣaṭkauśikaṃ samudbhūtaṃ bhajatyeṣa kalevaram
vicārataḥ satāṃ duḥkhaṃ strīsaṃsargādibhīrdvijāḥ
duḥkhenaikena vai duḥkhaṃ praśāmyantīha duḥkhinaḥ
tasmādvicārato nāsti saṃyogādapi vai nṛṇām
paiśāce rākṣase duḥkhaṃ yākṣe caiva vacāritaḥ
prājāpatye tathā brāhme prākṛte pauruṣe tathā
kṣayasātiśayādyaistu duḥkhairduḥkhāni suvratāḥ
tāni bhāgyānyaśuddhāni saṃtyajecca dhanāni ca
tasmādaṣṭaguṇaṃ bhogaṃ tathā ṣoḍaśadhā sthitam
caturviśatprakāreṇa saṃsthitaṃ cāpi suvratāḥ
dvātriṃśadbhedamanaghāścatvāriṃśadguṇaṃ punaḥ
tathāṣṭacatvāriṃścca ṣaṭpañcaṭāśatprakārataḥ
catuḥṣaṣṭividhaṃ caiva duḥkhameva vivekinaḥ
pārthivaṃ ca tathāpyaṃ ca taijasaṃ ca vicārataḥ
ābhimānikamapyevaṃ bauddhaṃ prākṛtameva ca
duḥkhameva na saṃdeho yogināṃ brahmavādinām
vartamānāni duḥkhāni bhaviṣyāṇi yathātatham
doṣaduṣṭeṣu deśeṣu duḥkhāni vividhāni ca
kṣudvyādheḥ parihārārthaṃ na sukhāyānnamucyate
yathetareṣāṃ rogāṇāmauṣadhaṃ na sukhāya tat
śītoṣṇavātavarṣādyaistattatkāleṣu dehinām
duḥkhameva na saṃdeho na jānānti hyapaṇḍitāḥ
svargepyevaṃ muniśreṣṭhā hyaviśuddhakṣayādibhiḥ
rogaurnānāvidhairgrastā rāgadveṣabhayādibhiḥ
puṇyavṛkṣakṣayāttadvadgāṃ patanti divaukasaḥ
narake duḥkhamevātra nākāṇāṃ niṣevaṇāt
vihitākaraṇāccaiva varṇināṃ munipuṅgavāḥ
evaṃ yatirdhyānaparo mahātmā saṃsārabhīto na labheta nidrām
kīṭapakṣimṛgāṇāṃ ca paśūnāṃ gajavājinām
dṛṣṭamevāsukhaṃ tasmāt tyajataḥ sukhamuttamam
vaimānikānāmapyevaṃ duḥkhaṃ kalpādhikāriṇām
sthānābhimānināṃ caiva manvādīnāṃ ca suvratāḥ
devānāṃ caiva daityānāmanyonyavijigīṣayā
duḥkhameva nṛpāṇāṃ ca rākṣasānāṃ jagattraye
śramārthamāśramaścāpi varṇānāṃ paramārthataḥ
āśramairna ca devaiś ca yajñaḥ sāṃkhyairvratais tathā
bhasmaśāyī bhavennityaṃ vrate pāśupate budhaḥ
pañcārthajñānasaṃpannaḥ śivatattve samāhitaḥ
pañcārthayogasaṃpanno duḥkhāntaṃ vrajate sudhīḥ
jyotiṣaṃ cāparā vidyā parākṣaramiti sthitam
tadadṛśyaṃ tadagrāhyamagotraṃ tadavarṇakam
tadacakṣustadaśrotraṃ tadapāṇi apādakam
tadajātamabhūtaṃ ca tadaśabdaṃ dvijottamāḥ
avyayaṃ cāpratiṣṭhaṃ ca tannityaṃ sarvagaṃ vibhum
mahāntaṃ tadgṛhaṃ taṃ ca tadajaṃ cinmayaṃ dvijāḥ
asaṃvṛtaṃ tadātmaikaṃ parā vidyā na cānyathā
matto nānyaditīkṣeta manovākpāṇibhis tathā
sarvamātmani saṃpaśyetsaccāsacca samāhitaḥ
adhodṛṣṭyā vitastyāṃ tu nābhyāmuparitiṣṭhati
hṛdayaṃ tadvijānīyādviśvasyāyatanaṃ mahat
chidrāṇi ca diśo yasya prāṇādyāś ca pratiṣṭhitāḥ
prāṇādyaiścaiva saṃyuktaḥ paśyate bahudhā kramāt
daśaprāṇavahā nāḍyaḥ pratyekaṃ munipuṅgavāḥ
netrasthañjāgrataṃ vidyātkaṇṭhe svapnaṃ samādiśet
suṣuptaṃ hṛdayasthaṃ tu turīyaṃ mūrdhani sthitam
jāgre brahmā ca viṣṇuś ca svapne caiva yathākramāt
